            		local v41 = v_u_8:GetUserCFrame(Enum.UserCFrame.RightHand)
            		local v42 = v37.Position - v40.Position
            		local v43 = v37.Position - v41.Position
            		local v44 = v42.X
            		local v45 = v42.Z
            		local v46 = -math.atan2(v44, v45)

            		local v48 = v43.Z

            			v49 = v49 - 6.283185307179586
            		end
            		local v50 = (v46 + v49 / 2 + 12.566370614359172) % 6.283185307179586

            		else

            		end

            		end

            		p36.currentTorsoAngle = v55 + p36.currentTorsoAngle
            	else
            		p36.currentTorsoAngle = v39
            	end
            	return CFrame.new(v37.Position) * CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(0, -p36.currentTorsoAngle, 0)
            end
            function v_u_1.GetActiveController(p57) -- name: GetActiveController
            	return p57.activeController

            function v_u_1.UpdateActiveControlModuleEnabled(p_u_58) -- name: UpdateActiveControlModuleEnabled
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_2, (copy) v_u_17, (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_20, (copy) v_u_19, (copy) v_u_12, (copy) v_u_21, (copy) v_u_5
            	local function v59()
            		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_58, (ref) v_u_17, (ref) v_u_4, (ref) v_u_20, (ref) v_u_19, (ref) v_u_12, (ref) v_u_21, (ref) v_u_2
            		if p_u_58.touchControlFrame and (not v_u_17 or v_u_4.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch) and (p_u_58.activeControlModule == v_u_20 or (p_u_58.activeControlModule == v_u_19 or p_u_58.activeControlModule == v_u_12)) then
            			if not p_u_58.controllers[v_u_21] then
            				p_u_58.controllers[v_u_21] = v_u_21.new()
            			end
            			p_u_58.touchJumpController = p_u_58.controllers[v_u_21]
            			p_u_58.touchJumpController:Enable(true, p_u_58.touchControlFrame)
            		elseif p_u_58.touchJumpController then
            			p_u_58.touchJumpController:Enable(false)
            		end
            		if p_u_58.activeControlModule == v_u_20 then
            			p_u_58.activeController:Enable(true, v_u_2.LocalPlayer.DevComputerMovementMode == Enum.DevComputerMovementMode.UserChoice, p_u_58.touchJumpController)
            			return
            		elseif p_u_58.touchControlFrame then
            			p_u_58.activeController:Enable(true, p_u_58.touchControlFrame)
            		else
            			p_u_58.activeController:Enable(true)
            		end
            	end
            	if p_u_58.activeController then
            		if p_u_58.controlsEnabled then
            			local v60
            			if v_u_17 then
            				v60 = v_u_4.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch
            			else
            				v60 = v_u_4.TouchEnabled
            			end
            			if v_u_5.TouchControlsEnabled or (not v60 or p_u_58.activeControlModule ~= v_u_20 and (p_u_58.activeControlModule ~= v_u_19 and p_u_58.activeControlModule ~= v_u_12)) then
            				v59()
            			else
            				p_u_58.activeController:Enable(false)
            				if p_u_58.touchJumpController then
            					p_u_58.touchJumpController:Enable(false)
            				end
            				if p_u_58.moveFunction then
            					p_u_58.moveFunction(v_u_2.LocalPlayer, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), true)
            				end
            			end
            		else
            			p_u_58.activeController:Enable(false)
            			if p_u_58.touchJumpController then
            				p_u_58.touchJumpController:Enable(false)
            			end
            			if p_u_58.moveFunction then
            				p_u_58.moveFunction(v_u_2.LocalPlayer, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), true)
            			end
            			return
            		end
            	else
            		return
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.Enable(p61, p62) -- name: Enable

            	if p61.controlsEnabled == v63 then

            function v_u_1.SelectComputerMovementModule(_) -- name: SelectComputerMovementModule
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_2, (copy) v_u_17, (copy) v_u_11, (copy) v_u_10, (copy) v_u_25, (ref) v_u_26, (copy) v_u_7, (copy) v_u_20, (copy) v_u_24
            	if v_u_4.KeyboardEnabled or v_u_4.GamepadEnabled then

            				if v_u_4.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Gamepad then
            					v65 = v_u_11
            				elseif v_u_4.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.KeyboardAndMouse then
            					v65 = v_u_10
            				end
            			else

            			end
            			if v_u_7.ComputerMovementMode == Enum.ComputerMovementMode.ClickToMove and v65 == v_u_10 then
            				v65 = v_u_20
            			end
            		else
            			v65 = v_u_24[v66]
            			if not v65 and v66 ~= Enum.DevComputerMovementMode.Scriptable then
            				warn("No character control module is associated with DevComputerMovementMode ", v66)
            			end
            		end
            		if v65 then
            			return v65, true
            		elseif v66 == Enum.DevComputerMovementMode.Scriptable then
            			return nil, true
            		else
            			return nil, false
            		end
            	else
            		return nil, false
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.SelectTouchModule(_) -- name: SelectTouchModule

            end
            local function v_u_71() -- name: getGamepadRightThumbstickPosition
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4
            	local v69 = v_u_4:GetGamepadState(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1)
            	for _, v70 in pairs(v69) do
            		if v70.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick2 then
            			return v70.Position
            		end
            	end
            	return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            function v_u_1.calculateRawMoveVector(p72, p73, p74) -- name: calculateRawMoveVector
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_6, (copy) v_u_8, (copy) v_u_71
            	local v75 = v_u_6.CurrentCamera
            	if not v75 then
            		return p74
            	end
            	local v76 = v75.CFrame
            	if v_u_8.VREnabled and p73.RootPart then
            		v_u_8:GetUserCFrame(Enum.UserCFrame.Head)
            		local v77 = p72:GetEstimatedVRTorsoFrame()
            		if (v75.Focus.Position - v76.Position).Magnitude < 3 then
            			v76 = v76 * v77
            		else
            			v76 = v75.CFrame * (v77.Rotation + v77.Position * v75.HeadScale)
            		end
            	end
            	if p73:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
            		local _, _, _, v82, v79, v80, _, _, v81, _, _, v82 = v76:GetComponents()
            		if v81 >= 1 or v81 <= -1 then
            			v80 = -v79 * math.sign(v81)
            		end
            		local v83 = v82 * v82 + v80 * v80
            		local v84 = math.sqrt(v83)
            		local v85 = (v82 * p74.X + v80 * p74.Z) / v84
            		local v86 = (v82 * p74.Z - v80 * p74.X) / v84
            		return Vector3.new(v85, 0, v86)
            	end
            	if not v_u_8.VREnabled then
            		return v76:VectorToWorldSpace(p74)
            	end
            	local v87 = p74.X
            	local v88 = p74.Z
            	local v89 = Vector3.new(v87, 0, v88)
            	if v89.Magnitude < 0.01 then
            		return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            	end
            	local v90 = -v_u_71().Y * 1.3962634015954636
            	local v91 = -v89.X
            	local v92 = -v89.Z
            	local v93 = math.atan2(v91, v92)
            	local _, v94, _ = v76:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
            	local v95 = v93 + v94
            	return CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(v90, v95, 0).LookVector
            end
            function v_u_1.OnRenderStepped(p96, p97) -- name: OnRenderStepped
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_11, (copy) v_u_8, (copy) v_u_2
            	if p96.activeController and (p96.activeController.enabled and p96.humanoid) then
            		local v98 = p96.activeController:GetMoveVector()
            		local v99 = p96.activeController:IsMoveVectorCameraRelative()
            		local v100 = p96:GetClickToMoveController()
            		if p96.activeController == v100 then
            			v100:OnRenderStepped(p97)
            		elseif v98.magnitude > 0 then
            			v100:CleanupPath()
            		else
            			v100:OnRenderStepped(p97)
            			v98 = v100:GetMoveVector()
            			v99 = v100:IsMoveVectorCameraRelative()
            		end
            		if p96.vehicleController then
            			local v101
            			v98, v101 = p96.vehicleController:Update(v98, v99, p96.activeControlModule == v_u_11)
            		end
            		if v99 then
            			v98 = p96:calculateRawMoveVector(p96.humanoid, v98)
            		end
            		p96.inputMoveVector = v98
            		if v_u_8.VREnabled then
            			v98 = p96:updateVRMoveVector(v98)
            		end
            		p96.moveFunction(v_u_2.LocalPlayer, v98, false)
            		local v102 = p96.humanoid
            		local v103 = not p96.activeController:GetIsJumping() and p96.touchJumpController
            		if v103 then
            			v103 = p96.touchJumpController:GetIsJumping()
            		end
            		v102.Jump = v103
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.updateVRMoveVector(p104, p105) -- name: updateVRMoveVector
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_8
            	local v106 = workspace.CurrentCamera
            	local v107 = (v106.Focus.Position - v106.CFrame.Position).Magnitude < 5
            	if p105.Magnitude ~= 0 or (not v107 or (not v_u_8.AvatarGestures or (not p104.humanoid or p104.humanoid.Sit))) then
            		return p105
            	end
            	local v108 = v_u_8:GetUserCFrame(Enum.UserCFrame.Head)
            	local v109 = v108.Rotation + v108.Position * v106.HeadScale
            	local v110 = -0.7 * p104.humanoid.RootPart.Size.Y / 2
            	local v111 = (v106.CFrame * v109 * CFrame.new(0, v110, 0)).Position - p104.humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.Position
            	local v112 = v111.x
            	local v113 = v111.z
            	return Vector3.new(v112, 0, v113)
            end

            function v_u_1.OnCharacterAdded(p_u_117, p118) -- name: OnCharacterAdded
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_17
            	p_u_117.humanoid = p118:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            		p_u_117.humanoidSeatedConn:Disconnect()

            	end

            		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_117
            		p_u_117:OnHumanoidSeated(p119, p120)
            	end)
            	if v_u_17 then
            		p_u_117:UpdateMovementMode()
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnCharacterRemoving(p121, _) -- name: OnCharacterRemoving

            end
            function v_u_1.UpdateTouchGuiVisibility(p122) -- name: UpdateTouchGuiVisibility
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_17, (copy) v_u_5, (copy) v_u_4
            	local v123
            	if v_u_17 then
            		v123 = p122.humanoid and v_u_5.TouchControlsEnabled
            		if v123 then
            			v123 = v_u_4.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch
            		end
            		if v123 and not p122.touchGui then
            			p122:CreateTouchGuiContainer()
            		end
            	else
            		v123 = p122.humanoid
            		if v123 then
            			v123 = v_u_5.TouchControlsEnabled
            		end
            	end
            	if p122.touchGui then
            		p122.touchGui.Enabled = v123 and true or false
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.SwitchToController(p124, p125) -- name: SwitchToController
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_23
            	if p125 then
            		if not p124.controllers[p125] then
            			p124.controllers[p125] = p125.new(v_u_23)
            		end
            		if p124.activeController ~= p124.controllers[p125] then
            			if p124.activeController then
            				p124.activeController:Enable(false)
            			end
            			p124.activeController = p124.controllers[p125]
            			p124.activeControlModule = p125
            			p124:UpdateActiveControlModuleEnabled()
            		end
            	else
            		if p124.activeController then
            			p124.activeController:Enable(false)
            		end
            		p124.activeController = nil
            		p124.activeControlModule = nil
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.UpdateMovementMode(p126) -- name: UpdateMovementMode
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4
            	p126:UpdateTouchGuiVisibility()
            	if v_u_4.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch then
            		local v127, v128 = p126:SelectTouchModule()
            		if v128 and p126.touchControlFrame then
            			p126:SwitchToController(v127)
            			return
            		end
            	else
            		p126:SwitchToController((p126:SelectComputerMovementModule()))
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnLastInputTypeChanged(p129, p130) -- name: OnLastInputTypeChanged
            	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_26, (copy) v_u_25
            	if v_u_26 == p130 then
            		warn("LastInputType Change listener called with current type.")
            	end
            	v_u_26 = p130
            	if v_u_26 == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            		local v131, v132 = p129:SelectTouchModule()
            		if v132 then
            			while not p129.touchControlFrame do
            				wait()
            			end
            			p129:SwitchToController(v131)
            		end
            	elseif v_u_25[v_u_26] ~= nil then
            		local v133 = p129:SelectComputerMovementModule()
            		if v133 then
            			p129:SwitchToController(v133)
            		end
            	end
            	p129:UpdateTouchGuiVisibility()
            end
            function v_u_1.OnComputerMovementModeChange(p134) -- name: OnComputerMovementModeChange

            	if v136 then
            		p134:SwitchToController(v135)
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnTouchMovementModeChange(p137) -- name: OnTouchMovementModeChange
            	local v138, v139 = p137:SelectTouchModule()
            	if v139 then
            		while not p137.touchControlFrame do
            			wait()
            		end
            		p137:SwitchToController(v138)
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.CreateTouchGuiContainer(p140) -- name: CreateTouchGuiContainer

            	end
            end

              function v_u_19.FadeThumbstick(p30, p31) -- name: FadeThumbstick
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_10, (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_2
              	if p31 or not p30.moveTouchObject then
              		if p30.isFirstTouch then
              			return
              		else
              			if p30.startImageFadeTween then
              				p30.startImageFadeTween:Cancel()
              			end
              			if p30.endImageFadeTween then
              				p30.endImageFadeTween:Cancel()
              			end
              			for v32 = 1, #p30.middleImages do
              				if p30.middleImageFadeTweens[v32] then
              					p30.middleImageFadeTweens[v32]:Cancel()
              				end
              			end
              			if p31 then
              				p30.startImageFadeTween = v_u_10:Create(p30.startImage, v_u_4, {
              					["ImageTransparency"] = 0
              				})
              				p30.startImageFadeTween:Play()
              				p30.endImageFadeTween = v_u_10:Create(p30.endImage, v_u_4, {
              					["ImageTransparency"] = 0.2
              				})
              				p30.endImageFadeTween:Play()
              				for v33 = 1, #p30.middleImages do
              					p30.middleImageFadeTweens[v33] = v_u_10:Create(p30.middleImages[v33], v_u_4, {
              						["ImageTransparency"] = v_u_2[v33]
              					})
              					p30.middleImageFadeTweens[v33]:Play()
              				end
              			else
              				p30.startImageFadeTween = v_u_10:Create(p30.startImage, v_u_4, {

              function v_u_19.DoMove(p46, p47) -- name: DoMove
              	local v48
              	if p47.Magnitude < p46.radiusOfDeadZone then
              		v48 = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              	else

              		local v50 = (p46.radiusOfMaxSpeed - p47.Magnitude) / p46.radiusOfMaxSpeed

              end

              			return v14
              		end
              	end
              end
              function v_u_4.setCurrentPoints(p23) -- name: setCurrentPoints
              	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_9
              	if typeof(p23) == "table" then
              		v_u_9 = p23
              	else
              		v_u_9 = {}
              	end
              end
              function v_u_4.clearRenderedPath() -- name: clearRenderedPath
              	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_10, (ref) v_u_7, (copy) v_u_8, (copy) v_u_5
              	for _, v24 in ipairs(v_u_10) do
              		v24.Parent = nil
              		v_u_7 = v_u_7 + 1
              		v_u_8[v_u_7] = v24
              	end
              	v_u_10 = {}
              	v_u_5.Parent = nil
              end
              function v_u_4.renderPath() -- name: renderPath
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4, (ref) v_u_9, (ref) v_u_10, (copy) v_u_22, (copy) v_u_5
              	v_u_4.clearRenderedPath()
              	if v_u_9 and #v_u_9 ~= 0 then
              		local v25 = #v_u_9
              		v_u_10[1] = v_u_22(v_u_9[v25], true)
              		if v_u_10[1] then
              			local v26 = 0
              			while true do
              				local v27 = v_u_9[v25]
              				local v28 = v_u_9[v25 - 1]
              				if v25 < 2 then
              					break
              				end
              				local v29 = v28 - v27
              				local v30 = v29.magnitude
              				if v30 < v26 then
              					v26 = v26 - v30
              					v25 = v25 - 1
              				else
              					local v31 = v_u_22(v27 + v29.unit * v26, false)
              					if v31 then
              						v_u_10[#v_u_10 + 1] = v31
              					end
              					v26 = v26 + v_u_4.spacing
              				end
              			end
              			v_u_5.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera
              		end
              	else
              		return
              	end
              end
              return v_u_4

              		p11.jumpButton.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(1, 146)

              function v_u_7.EnableButton(p_u_12, p13) -- name: EnableButton
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_5, (copy) v_u_1
              	if p13 == p_u_12._active then
              		if not v_u_5 then
              			p_u_12:_reset()
              		end
              	else
              		if p13 then
              			if not p_u_12.jumpButton then
              				p_u_12:Create()
              			end
              			p_u_12.jumpButton.Visible = true
              			p_u_12._connectionUtil:trackConnection("JUMP_INPUT_ENDED", p_u_12.jumpButton.InputEnded:Connect(function(p14)
              				-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_12
              				if p14 == p_u_12.touchObject then
              					p_u_12:_reset()
              				end
              			end))
              			p_u_12._connectionUtil:trackConnection("MENU_OPENED", v_u_1.MenuOpened:Connect(function()
              				-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_12
              				if p_u_12.touchObject then
              					p_u_12:_reset()
              				end
              			end))
              		else
              			if p_u_12.jumpButton then
              				p_u_12.jumpButton.Visible = false
              			end
              			p_u_12._connectionUtil:disconnect("JUMP_INPUT_ENDED")
              			p_u_12._connectionUtil:disconnect("MENU_OPENED")
              		end
              		p_u_12:_reset()
              		p_u_12._active = p13
              	end
              end
              function v_u_7.UpdateEnabled(p15) -- name: UpdateEnabled
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_5
              	local v16 = v_u_4.getChild("Humanoid", "Humanoid")
              	if v_u_5 then
              		if v16 and p15.externallyEnabled and (v16.UseJumpPower and v16.JumpPower > 0 or not v16.UseJumpPower and v16.JumpHeight > 0) and v16:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) then
              			p15:EnableButton(true)
              		else
              			p15:EnableButton(false)
              		end
              	elseif v16 and (p15.externallyEnabled and (v16.JumpPower > 0 and v16:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping))) then
              		p15:EnableButton(true)
              	else
              		p15:EnableButton(false)
              	end
              end
              function v_u_7._setupConfigurations(p_u_17) -- name: _setupConfigurations
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_5
              	local function v_u_18() -- name: update
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_17
              		p_u_17:UpdateEnabled()
              	end
              	local v22 = v_u_4.onChild("Humanoid", "Humanoid", function(p19)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_17, (ref) v_u_5, (copy) v_u_18

              		if v_u_5 then
              			p_u_17:_reset()
              		end
              		p_u_17._connectionUtil:trackConnection("HUMANOID_JUMP_POWER", p19:GetPropertyChangedSignal("JumpPower"):Connect(v_u_18))

              			p_u_17._connectionUtil:trackConnection("HUMANOID_JUMP_HEIGHT", p19:GetPropertyChangedSignal("JumpHeight"):Connect(v_u_18))
              		end

              			-- upvalues: (ref) p_u_17
              			if p20 == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and p21 ~= p_u_17._active then
              				p_u_17:UpdateEnabled()
              			end
              		end))
              	end)
              	p_u_17._connectionUtil:trackConnection("HUMANOID", v22)
              end
              function v_u_7.Enable(p23, p24, p25) -- name: Enable
              	if p25 then
              		p23.parentUIFrame = p25
              	end
              	if p23.externallyEnabled == p24 then
              		return
              	else
              		p23.externallyEnabled = p24
              		p23:UpdateEnabled()
              		if p24 then

              		else
              			p23._connectionUtil:disconnectAll()
              		end
              	end
              end
              function v_u_7.Create(p_u_26) -- name: Create
              	if p_u_26.parentUIFrame then
              		if p_u_26.jumpButton then

              		end

              		end

              		local function v31() -- name: ResizeJumpButton
              			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_26
              			local v27 = p_u_26.parentUIFrame.AbsoluteSize.x
              			local v28 = p_u_26.parentUIFrame.AbsoluteSize.y
              			local v29 = math.min(v27, v28) <= 500
              			local v30 = v29 and 70 or 120
              			p_u_26.jumpButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, v30, 0, v30)
              			p_u_26.jumpButton.Position = v29 and UDim2.new(1, -(v30 * 1.5 - 10), 1, -v30 - 20) or UDim2.new(1, -(v30 * 1.5 - 10), 1, -v30 * 1.75)
              		end
              		v31()
              		p_u_26.absoluteSizeChangedConn = p_u_26.parentUIFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(v31)
              		p_u_26.touchObject = nil

              				p_u_26.jumpButton.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(146, 146)
              				p_u_26.isJumping = true
              			end
              		end)

          - [ModuleScript] TouchThumbstick

              function v_u_4.Enable(p8, p9, p10) -- name: Enable
              	if p9 == nil then
              		return false
              	end
              	local v11 = p9 and true or false
              	if p8.enabled == v11 then
              		return true
              	end
              	p8.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              	p8.isJumping = false
              	if v11 then
              		if not p8.thumbstickFrame then
              			p8:Create(p10)
              		end
              		p8.thumbstickFrame.Visible = true
              	else
              		p8.thumbstickFrame.Visible = false
              		p8:OnInputEnded()
              	end
              	p8.enabled = v11
              end
              function v_u_4.OnInputEnded(p12) -- name: OnInputEnded
              	p12.thumbstickFrame.Position = p12.screenPos
              	p12.stickImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, p12.thumbstickFrame.Size.X.Offset / 2 - p12.thumbstickSize / 4, 0, p12.thumbstickFrame.Size.Y.Offset / 2 - p12.thumbstickSize / 4)
              	p12.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              	p12.isJumping = false
              	p12.thumbstickFrame.Position = p12.screenPos
              	p12.moveTouchObject = nil
              end
              function v_u_4.Create(p_u_13, p_u_14) -- name: Create
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_2, (copy) v_u_1
              	if p_u_13.thumbstickFrame then
              		p_u_13.thumbstickFrame:Destroy()
              		p_u_13.thumbstickFrame = nil
              		if p_u_13.onTouchMovedConn then
              			p_u_13.onTouchMovedConn:Disconnect()
              			p_u_13.onTouchMovedConn = nil
              		end
              		if p_u_13.onTouchEndedConn then
              			p_u_13.onTouchEndedConn:Disconnect()
              			p_u_13.onTouchEndedConn = nil
              		end
              		if p_u_13.absoluteSizeChangedConn then
              			p_u_13.absoluteSizeChangedConn:Disconnect()
              			p_u_13.absoluteSizeChangedConn = nil
              		end
              	end
              	p_u_13.thumbstickFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
              	p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Name = "ThumbstickFrame"
              	p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Active = true
              	p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Visible = false
              	p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
              	local v_u_15 = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
              	v_u_15.Name = "OuterImage"
              	v_u_15.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/TouchControlsSheet.png"
              	v_u_15.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new()
              	v_u_15.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(220, 220)
              	v_u_15.BackgroundTransparency = 1
              	v_u_15.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
              	p_u_13.stickImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
              	p_u_13.stickImage.Name = "StickImage"
              	p_u_13.stickImage.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/TouchControlsSheet.png"

              	p_u_13.stickImage.ImageRectSize = Vector2.new(111, 111)
              	p_u_13.stickImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
              	p_u_13.stickImage.ZIndex = 2
              	local function v19() -- name: ResizeThumbstick
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_14, (copy) p_u_13, (copy) v_u_15

              		local v17 = p_u_14.AbsoluteSize.Y

              		p_u_13.thumbstickSize = v18 and 70 or 120
              		p_u_13.screenPos = v18 and UDim2.new(0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize / 2 - 10, 1, -p_u_13.thumbstickSize - 20) or UDim2.new(0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize / 2, 1, -p_u_13.thumbstickSize * 1.75)
              		p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize, 0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize)
              		p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Position = p_u_13.screenPos
              		v_u_15.Size = UDim2.new(0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize, 0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize)
              		p_u_13.stickImage.Size = UDim2.new(0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize / 2, 0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize / 2)
              		p_u_13.stickImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize / 2 - p_u_13.thumbstickSize / 4, 0, p_u_13.thumbstickSize / 2 - p_u_13.thumbstickSize / 4)
              	end
              	v19()
              	p_u_13.absoluteSizeChangedConn = p_u_14:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(v19)
              	v_u_15.Parent = p_u_13.thumbstickFrame
              	p_u_13.stickImage.Parent = p_u_13.thumbstickFrame
              	local v_u_20 = nil
              	local function v_u_27(p21) -- name: MoveStick
              		-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_20, (copy) p_u_13
              		local v22 = Vector2.new(p21.X - v_u_20.X, p21.Y - v_u_20.Y)
              		local v23 = v22.magnitude
              		local v24 = p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.X / 2
              		if p_u_13.isFollowStick and v24 < v23 then
              			local v25 = v22.unit * v24
              			p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, p21.X - p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.X / 2 - v25.X, 0, p21.Y - p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2 - v25.Y)
              		else
              			local v26 = math.min(v23, v24)
              			v22 = v22.unit * v26
              		end
              		p_u_13.stickImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, v22.X + p_u_13.stickImage.AbsoluteSize.X / 2, 0, v22.Y + p_u_13.stickImage.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2)
              	end
              	p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(p28)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_13, (ref) v_u_20
              		if not p_u_13.moveTouchObject and (p28.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and p28.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin) then
              			p_u_13.moveTouchObject = p28
              			p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, p28.Position.X - p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Size.X.Offset / 2, 0, p28.Position.Y - p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Size.Y.Offset / 2)

              			local v31 = v30.magnitude
              			local v32
              			if v31 < 0.05 then
              				v32 = Vector3.new()

              				local v35 = v33 * math.min(1, v34)
              				local v36 = v35.X
              				local v37 = v35.Y
              				v32 = Vector3.new(v36, 0, v37)
              			end
              			p_u_13.moveVector = v32
              			v_u_27(p29.Position)
              		end
              	end)
              	p_u_13.onTouchEndedConn = v_u_2.TouchEnded:Connect(function(p38, _)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_13
              		if p38 == p_u_13.moveTouchObject then
              			p_u_13:OnInputEnded()
              		end
              	end)
              	v_u_1.MenuOpened:Connect(function()
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_13
              		if p_u_13.moveTouchObject then
              			p_u_13:OnInputEnded()
              		end
              	end)
              	p_u_13.thumbstickFrame.Parent = p_u_14
              end
              return v_u_4

            ---------------------------------
          - [ModuleScript] Gamepad
            --- [СКОПИРОВАННЫЙ КОД СРИПТА] ---
              local v_u_1 = game:GetService("UserInputService")
              local v_u_2 = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
              script.Parent.Parent:WaitForChild("CommonUtils")
              local v_u_3 = Enum.UserInputType.None
              local v_u_4 = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("BaseCharacterController"))
              local v_u_5 = setmetatable({}, v_u_4)
              v_u_5.__index = v_u_5
              function v_u_5.new(p6) -- name: new
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_5, (copy) v_u_3
              	local v7 = v_u_4.new()
              	local v8 = v_u_5
              	local v9 = setmetatable(v7, v8)
              	v9.CONTROL_ACTION_PRIORITY = p6
              	v9.forwardValue = 0
              	v9.backwardValue = 0
              	v9.leftValue = 0
              	v9.rightValue = 0
              	v9.activeGamepad = v_u_3
              	v9.gamepadConnectedConn = nil
              	v9.gamepadDisconnectedConn = nil
              	return v9
              end
              function v_u_5.Enable(p10, p11) -- name: Enable
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_3
              	if p11 == p10.enabled then
              		return true
              	end
              	p10.forwardValue = 0
              	p10.backwardValue = 0
              	p10.leftValue = 0
              	p10.rightValue = 0
              	p10.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              	p10.isJumping = false
              	if p11 then
              		p10.activeGamepad = p10:GetHighestPriorityGamepad()
              		if p10.activeGamepad == v_u_3 then
              			return false
              		end
              		p10:BindContextActions()
              		p10:ConnectGamepadConnectionListeners()
              	else
              		p10:UnbindContextActions()
              		p10:DisconnectGamepadConnectionListeners()
              		p10.activeGamepad = v_u_3
              	end
              	p10.enabled = p11
              	return true
              end
              function v_u_5.GetHighestPriorityGamepad(_) -- name: GetHighestPriorityGamepad
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1, (copy) v_u_3
              	local v12 = v_u_1:GetConnectedGamepads()
              	local v13 = v_u_3
              	for _, v14 in pairs(v12) do
              		if v14.Value < v13.Value then
              			v13 = v14
              		end
              	end
              	return v13
              end
              function v_u_5.BindContextActions(p_u_15) -- name: BindContextActions
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_2
              	if p_u_15.activeGamepad == v_u_3 then
              		return false
              	end
              	v_u_2:BindActivate(p_u_15.activeGamepad, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2)
              	v_u_2:BindActionAtPriority("jumpAction", function(_, p16, _)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_15
              		p_u_15.isJumping = p16 == Enum.UserInputState.Begin
              		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
              	end, false, p_u_15.CONTROL_ACTION_PRIORITY, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)
              	v_u_2:BindActionAtPriority("moveThumbstick", function(_, p17, p18)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_15
              		if p17 == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
              			p_u_15.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
              		end
              		if p_u_15.activeGamepad ~= p18.UserInputType then
              			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
              		end
              		if p18.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 then
              			if p18.Position.magnitude > 0.2 then
              				local v19 = p_u_15
              				local v20 = p18.Position.X
              				local v21 = -p18.Position.Y
              				v19.moveVector = Vector3.new(v20, 0, v21)
              			else
              				p_u_15.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              			end
              			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
              		end
              	end, false, p_u_15.CONTROL_ACTION_PRIORITY, Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1)
              	return true
              end
              function v_u_5.UnbindContextActions(p22) -- name: UnbindContextActions
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_2
              	if p22.activeGamepad ~= v_u_3 then
              		v_u_2:UnbindActivate(p22.activeGamepad, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2)
              	end
              	v_u_2:UnbindAction("moveThumbstick")
              	v_u_2:UnbindAction("jumpAction")
              end
              function v_u_5.OnNewGamepadConnected(p23) -- name: OnNewGamepadConnected
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_3
              	local v24 = p23:GetHighestPriorityGamepad()
              	if v24 == p23.activeGamepad then
              		return
              	elseif v24 == v_u_3 then
              		warn("Gamepad:OnNewGamepadConnected found no connected gamepads")
              		p23:UnbindContextActions()
              	else
              		if p23.activeGamepad ~= v_u_3 then
              			p23:UnbindContextActions()
              		end
              		p23.activeGamepad = v24
              		p23:BindContextActions()
              	end
              end
              function v_u_5.OnCurrentGamepadDisconnected(p25) -- name: OnCurrentGamepadDisconnected
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_2
              	if p25.activeGamepad ~= v_u_3 then
              		v_u_2:UnbindActivate(p25.activeGamepad, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2)
              	end
              	local v26 = p25:GetHighestPriorityGamepad()
              	if p25.activeGamepad == v_u_3 or v26 ~= p25.activeGamepad then
              		if v26 == v_u_3 then
              			p25:UnbindContextActions()
              			p25.activeGamepad = v_u_3
              		else
              			p25.activeGamepad = v26
              			v_u_2:BindActivate(p25.activeGamepad, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2)
              		end
              	else
              		warn("Gamepad:OnCurrentGamepadDisconnected found the supposedly disconnected gamepad in connectedGamepads.")
              		p25:UnbindContextActions()
              		p25.activeGamepad = v_u_3
              		return
              	end
              end
              function v_u_5.ConnectGamepadConnectionListeners(p_u_27) -- name: ConnectGamepadConnectionListeners
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1
              	p_u_27.gamepadConnectedConn = v_u_1.GamepadConnected:Connect(function(_)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_27
              		p_u_27:OnNewGamepadConnected()
              	end)
              	p_u_27.gamepadDisconnectedConn = v_u_1.GamepadDisconnected:Connect(function(p28)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_27
              		if p_u_27.activeGamepad == p28 then
              			p_u_27:OnCurrentGamepadDisconnected()
              		end
              	end)
              end
              function v_u_5.DisconnectGamepadConnectionListeners(p29) -- name: DisconnectGamepadConnectionListeners
              	if p29.gamepadConnectedConn then
              		p29.gamepadConnectedConn:Disconnect()
              		p29.gamepadConnectedConn = nil
              	end
              	if p29.gamepadDisconnectedConn then
              		p29.gamepadDisconnectedConn:Disconnect()
              		p29.gamepadDisconnectedConn = nil
              	end
              end
              return v_u_5

              function v_u_2.Enable(p19, p20, p21) -- name: Enable
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1
              	if p20 ~= p19.enabled or p21 ~= p19.vehicleSeat then
              		p19.enabled = p20
              		p19.vehicleMoveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              		if p20 then
              			if p21 then
              				p19.vehicleSeat = p21
              				p19:SetupAutoPilot()
              				p19:BindContextActions()
              				return
              			end
              		else
              			v_u_1:UnbindAction("throttleAccel")
              			v_u_1:UnbindAction("throttleDeccel")
              			v_u_1:UnbindAction("arrowSteerRight")

              			p19.vehicleSeat = nil
              		end
              	end
              end
              function v_u_2.OnThrottleAccel(p22, _, p23, _) -- name: OnThrottleAccel
              	if p23 == Enum.UserInputState.End or p23 == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
              		p22.acceleration = 0
              	else
              		p22.acceleration = -1
              	end
              	p22.throttle = p22.acceleration + p22.decceleration
              end
              function v_u_2.OnThrottleDeccel(p24, _, p25, _) -- name: OnThrottleDeccel
              	if p25 == Enum.UserInputState.End or p25 == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
              		p24.decceleration = 0
              	else
              		p24.decceleration = 1
              	end
              	p24.throttle = p24.acceleration + p24.decceleration
              end
              function v_u_2.OnSteerRight(p26, _, p27, _) -- name: OnSteerRight
              	if p27 == Enum.UserInputState.End or p27 == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
              		p26.turningRight = 0
              	else
              		p26.turningRight = 1
              	end
              	p26.steer = p26.turningRight + p26.turningLeft
              end
              function v_u_2.OnSteerLeft(p28, _, p29, _) -- name: OnSteerLeft
              	if p29 == Enum.UserInputState.End or p29 == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
              		p28.turningLeft = 0
              	else
              		p28.turningLeft = -1
              	end
              	p28.steer = p28.turningRight + p28.turningLeft
              end
              function v_u_2.Update(p30, p31, p32, _) -- name: Update
              	if not p30.vehicleSeat then
              		return p31, false
              	end
              	if not p32 then
              		local v33 = p30.vehicleSeat.Occupant.RootPart.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(p31)
              		p30.vehicleSeat.ThrottleFloat = p30:ComputeThrottle(v33)
              		p30.vehicleSeat.SteerFloat = p30:ComputeSteer(v33)
              		return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), true
              	end
              	local v34 = p30.steer
              	local v35 = p30.throttle
              	local v36 = p31 + Vector3.new(v34, 0, v35)
              	p30.vehicleSeat.ThrottleFloat = -v36.Z
              	p30.vehicleSeat.SteerFloat = v36.X
              	return v36, true
              end
              function v_u_2.ComputeThrottle(_, p37) -- name: ComputeThrottle
              	return p37 == Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) and 0 or -p37.Z
              end
              function v_u_2.ComputeSteer(p38, p39) -- name: ComputeSteer
              	if p39 == Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) then
              		return 0
              	end
              	local v40 = -p39.x
              	local v41 = -p39.z
              	return -math.atan2(v40, v41) * 57.29577951308232 / p38.autoPilot.MaxSteeringAngle
              end
              function v_u_2.SetupAutoPilot(p42) -- name: SetupAutoPilot
              	p42.autoPilot.MaxSpeed = p42.vehicleSeat.MaxSpeed
              	p42.autoPilot.MaxSteeringAngle = 35
              end
              return v_u_2

              local function v_u_122(p58) -- name: getCollidableExtentsSize
              	if p58 == nil or p58.PrimaryPart == nil then
              		return
              	else
              		assert(p58, "")
              		local v59 = p58.PrimaryPart
              		assert(v59, "")
              		local v60 = p58.PrimaryPart.CFrame:Inverse()
              		local v61 = Vector3.new(inf, inf, inf)
              		local v62 = Vector3.new(-inf, -inf, -inf)
              		for _, v63 in pairs(p58:GetDescendants()) do
              			if v63:IsA("BasePart") and v63.CanCollide then
              				local v64 = v60 * v63.CFrame
              				local v65 = v63.Size.X / 2

              	function v_u_126.IsCurrentWaypointReached(_) -- name: IsCurrentWaypointReached
              		-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_126
              		local v155
              		if v_u_126.CurrentWaypointPlaneNormal == Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) then
              			v155 = true

              	end
              	function v_u_126.OnPointReached(_, p158) -- name: OnPointReached
              		-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_126
              		if p158 and not v_u_126.Cancelled then

              					end

              	end
              	function v_u_126.MoveToNextWayPoint(_, p167, p168, p169) -- name: MoveToNextWayPoint

              	function v_u_126.Start(_, p173) -- name: Start
              		-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_126, (ref) v_u_25, (ref) v_u_18
              		if v_u_126.AgentCanFollowPath then

              					local v175 = v_u_126
              					local v176, v177 = v_u_25.CreatePathDisplay(v_u_126.pointList, v_u_126.OriginalTargetPoint)
              					v174.stopTraverseFunc = v176

              						v_u_126:OnPathInterrupted()

              						-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_126
              						v_u_126:OnPathInterrupted()
              					end)

              						-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_126
              						v_u_126:OnPathInterrupted()
              					end)
              					v_u_126.CurrentPoint = 1
              					v_u_126:OnPointReached(true)
              				else
              					v_u_126.PathFailed:Fire()

              						v_u_126.stopTraverseFunc()

              				end
              			end

              			return
              		end
              	end
              	local v180 = v_u_126.TargetPoint + v_u_126.TargetSurfaceNormal * 1.5

              			local v185 = v_u_24
              			if v185 then
              				v185 = v_u_24.Character
              			end

              			v182 = v_u_43
              		end
              		v181.FilterDescendantsInstances = v182
              		local v186 = v_u_11:Raycast(v180, Vector3.new(-0, -50, -0), v_u_26)
              		if v186 then
              			v_u_126.TargetPoint = v186.Position
              		end
              	else

              		local v188 = v_u_11
              		local v189
              		if v_u_43 then
              			v189 = v_u_43
              		else
              			v_u_43 = {}
              			local v190 = v_u_43
              			assert(v190, "")
              			local v191 = v_u_43
              			local v192 = v_u_24
              			if v192 then
              				v192 = v_u_24.Character
              			end
              			table.insert(v191, v192)
              			v189 = v_u_43
              		end
              		local v193, v194 = v188:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(v187, v189)
              		if v193 then
              			v_u_126.TargetPoint = v194
              		end
              	end
              	v_u_126:ComputePath()
              	return v_u_126
              end
              local function v_u_198(p196) -- name: GetEquippedTool
              	if p196 ~= nil then

              		end
              	end
              end
              local v_u_199 = nil
              local v_u_200 = nil
              local v_u_201 = nil
              local function v_u_210(p202, p_u_203, p_u_204, p_u_205, p_u_206) -- name: HandleMoveTo
              	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201, (copy) v_u_198, (ref) v_u_19, (copy) v_u_25
              	if v_u_199 then
              		if v_u_199 then
              			v_u_199:Cancel()
              			v_u_199 = nil
              		end
              		if v_u_200 then
              			v_u_200:Disconnect()
              			v_u_200 = nil
              		end
              		if v_u_201 then
              			v_u_201:Disconnect()
              			v_u_201 = nil
              		end
              	end
              	v_u_199 = p202
              	p202:Start(p_u_206)
              	v_u_200 = p202.Finished.Event:Connect(function()
              		-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201, (copy) p_u_204, (ref) v_u_198, (copy) p_u_205
              		if v_u_199 then
              			v_u_199:Cancel()
              			v_u_199 = nil
              		end
              		if v_u_200 then
              			v_u_200:Disconnect()
              			v_u_200 = nil
              		end
              		if v_u_201 then
              			v_u_201:Disconnect()
              			v_u_201 = nil
              		end
              		local v207 = p_u_204 and v_u_198(p_u_205)
              		if v207 then
              			v207:Activate()
              		end
              	end)
              	v_u_201 = p202.PathFailed.Event:Connect(function()
              		-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201, (copy) p_u_206, (ref) v_u_19, (ref) v_u_25, (copy) p_u_203
              		if v_u_199 then
              			v_u_199:Cancel()
              			v_u_199 = nil
              		end
              		if v_u_200 then
              			v_u_200:Disconnect()
              			v_u_200 = nil
              		end
              		if v_u_201 then
              			v_u_201:Disconnect()
              			v_u_201 = nil
              		end
              		if p_u_206 == nil or p_u_206 then
              			local v208 = v_u_19
              			if v208 then
              				local v209 = v_u_199
              				if v209 then
              					v209 = v_u_199:IsActive()
              				end
              				v208 = not v209
              			end
              			if v208 then
              				v_u_25.PlayFailureAnimation()
              			end
              			v_u_25.DisplayFailureWaypoint(p_u_203)
              		end
              	end)
              end
              function OnTap(p211, p212, p213) -- name: OnTap
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_11, (copy) v_u_24, (copy) v_u_42, (copy) v_u_16, (ref) v_u_43, (copy) v_u_26, (copy) v_u_10, (copy) v_u_9, (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201, (copy) v_u_195, (copy) v_u_210, (ref) v_u_19, (copy) v_u_25, (copy) v_u_27, (copy) v_u_198
              	local v214 = v_u_11.CurrentCamera
              	local v215 = v_u_24.Character
              	local v216 = v_u_24
              	local v217
              	if v216 then
              		v217 = v216.Character
              	else
              		v217 = v216
              	end
              	local v218
              	if v217 then
              		v218 = v_u_42[v216]
              		if not v218 or v218.Parent ~= v217 then
              			v_u_42[v216] = nil
              			v218 = v217:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
              			if v218 then
              				v_u_42[v216] = v218
              			end
              		end
              	else
              		v218 = nil
              	end
              	local v219
              	if v218 == nil then
              		v219 = false
              	else
              		v219 = v218.Health > 0
              	end
              	if not v219 then
              		return
              	end
              	if #p211 ~= 1 and not p212 then
              		local v220 = #p211 >= 2 and (v214 and v_u_198(v215))
              		if v220 then
              			v220:Activate()
              		end
              		goto l21
              	end
              	if not v214 then
              		::l21::
              		return
              	end
              	local v221 = v214:ScreenPointToRay(p211[1].X, p211[1].Y)
              	if not v_u_16 then
              		local v222 = Ray.new(v221.Origin, v221.Direction * 1000)
              		local v223 = v_u_27.Raycast
              		local v224 = true
              		local v225
              		if v_u_43 then
              			v225 = v_u_43
              		else
              			v_u_43 = {}
              			local v226 = v_u_43
              			assert(v226, "")
              			local v227 = v_u_43
              			local v228 = v_u_24
              			if v228 then
              				v228 = v_u_24.Character
              			end
              			table.insert(v227, v228)
              			v225 = v_u_43
              		end
              		local v229, v230, v231 = v223(v222, v224, v225)
              		local v232, v233 = v_u_27.FindCharacterAncestor(v229)
              		if p213 and (v233 and (v_u_10:GetCore("AvatarContextMenuEnabled") and v_u_9:GetPlayerFromCharacter(v233.Parent))) then
              			if v_u_199 then
              				v_u_199:Cancel()
              				v_u_199 = nil
              			end
              			if v_u_200 then
              				v_u_200:Disconnect()
              				v_u_200 = nil
              			end
              			if v_u_201 then
              				v_u_201:Disconnect()
              				v_u_201 = nil
              			end
              			return
              		end
              		if p212 then
              			v232 = nil
              		else
              			p212 = v230
              		end
              		if p212 and v215 then
              			if v_u_199 then
              				v_u_199:Cancel()
              				v_u_199 = nil
              			end
              			if v_u_200 then
              				v_u_200:Disconnect()
              				v_u_200 = nil
              			end
              			if v_u_201 then
              				v_u_201:Disconnect()
              				v_u_201 = nil
              			end
              			local v234 = v_u_195(p212, v231)
              			if v234:IsValidPath() then
              				v_u_210(v234, p212, v232, v215)
              			else
              				v234:Cleanup()
              				if v_u_199 and v_u_199:IsActive() then
              					v_u_199:Cancel()
              				end
              				if v_u_19 then
              					v_u_25.PlayFailureAnimation()
              				end
              				v_u_25.DisplayFailureWaypoint(p212)
              			end
              		end
              		goto l21
              	end
              	local v235 = nil
              	local v236 = nil
              	local v237
              	if v_u_43 then
              		v237 = v_u_43
              	else
              		v_u_43 = {}
              		local v238 = v_u_43
              		assert(v238, "")
              		local v239 = v_u_43
              		local v240 = v_u_24
              		if v240 then
              			v240 = v_u_24.Character
              		end
              		table.insert(v239, v240)
              		v237 = v_u_43
              	end
              	if not v237 then
              		v237 = {}
              	end
              	while true do
              		local v241 = true
              		v_u_26.FilterDescendantsInstances = v237
              		local v242 = v_u_11:Raycast(v221.Origin, v221.Direction * 1000, v_u_26)
              		if v242 then
              			local v243 = v242.Instance
              			if not v243.CanCollide then
              				while true do
              					v235 = v243:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
              					local v244 = v243.Parent
              					if v235 or (not v244 or v244 == v_u_11) then
              						break
              					end
              					v243 = v244
              				end
              				if v235 then
              					v236 = v243
              				else
              					table.insert(v237, v244)
              					v241 = false
              					v236 = nil
              				end
              				goto l32
              			end
              		end
              		::l32::
              		if v241 then
              			if p213 and (v235 and (v_u_10:GetCore("AvatarContextMenuEnabled") and v_u_9:GetPlayerFromCharacter(v235.Parent))) then
              				if v_u_199 then
              					v_u_199:Cancel()
              					v_u_199 = nil
              				end
              				if v_u_200 then
              					v_u_200:Disconnect()
              					v_u_200 = nil
              				end
              				if v_u_201 then
              					v_u_201:Disconnect()
              					v_u_201 = nil
              				end
              				return
              			elseif v242 and v215 then
              				local v245 = v242.Position
              				if p212 then
              					v236 = nil
              				else
              					p212 = v245
              				end
              				if v_u_199 then
              					v_u_199:Cancel()
              					v_u_199 = nil
              				end
              				if v_u_200 then
              					v_u_200:Disconnect()
              					v_u_200 = nil
              				end
              				if v_u_201 then
              					v_u_201:Disconnect()
              					v_u_201 = nil
              				end
              				local v246 = v_u_195(p212, v242.Normal)
              				if v246:IsValidPath() then
              					v_u_210(v246, p212, v236, v215)
              				else
              					v246:Cleanup()
              					if v_u_199 and v_u_199:IsActive() then
              						v_u_199:Cancel()
              					end
              					if v_u_19 then
              						v_u_25.PlayFailureAnimation()
              					end
              					v_u_25.DisplayFailureWaypoint(p212)
              				end
              			else
              				return
              			end
              		end
              	end
              end
              local v_u_247 = require(script.Parent:WaitForChild("Keyboard"))
              local v_u_248 = setmetatable({}, v_u_247)
              v_u_248.__index = v_u_248
              function v_u_248.new(p249) -- name: new
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_247, (copy) v_u_248
              	local v250 = v_u_247.new(p249)
              	local v251 = v_u_248
              	local v252 = setmetatable(v250, v251)
              	v252.fingerTouches = {}
              	v252.numUnsunkTouches = 0
              	v252.mouse2DownTime = tick()
              	v252.mouse2DownPos = Vector2.new()
              	v252.mouse2UpTime = tick()
              	v252.keyboardMoveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              	v252.tapConn = nil
              	v252.inputBeganConn = nil
              	v252.inputChangedConn = nil
              	v252.inputEndedConn = nil
              	v252.humanoidDiedConn = nil
              	v252.characterChildAddedConn = nil
              	v252.onCharacterAddedConn = nil
              	v252.characterChildRemovedConn = nil
              	v252.renderSteppedConn = nil
              	v252.menuOpenedConnection = nil
              	v252.preferredInputChangedConnection = nil
              	v252.running = false
              	v252.wasdEnabled = false
              	return v252
              end
              function v_u_248.DisconnectEvents(p253) -- name: DisconnectEvents
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_17
              	local v254 = p253.tapConn
              	if v254 then
              		v254:Disconnect()
              	end
              	local v255 = p253.inputBeganConn
              	if v255 then
              		v255:Disconnect()
              	end

              	if v256 then
              		v256:Disconnect()
              	end
              	local v257 = p253.inputEndedConn
              	if v257 then
              		v257:Disconnect()
              	end
              	local v258 = p253.humanoidDiedConn
              	if v258 then
              		v258:Disconnect()
              	end
              	local v259 = p253.characterChildAddedConn
              	if v259 then
              		v259:Disconnect()
              	end
              	local v260 = p253.onCharacterAddedConn
              	if v260 then
              		v260:Disconnect()
              	end
              	local v261 = p253.renderSteppedConn
              	if v261 then
              		v261:Disconnect()
              	end

              	if v262 then
              		v262:Disconnect()

              	if v264 then

              end
              function v_u_248.OnTouchBegan(p265, p266, p267) -- name: OnTouchBegan
              	if p265.fingerTouches[p266] == nil and not p267 then
              		p265.numUnsunkTouches = p265.numUnsunkTouches + 1
              	end
              	p265.fingerTouches[p266] = p267
              end
              function v_u_248.OnTouchChanged(p268, p269, p270) -- name: OnTouchChanged
              	if p268.fingerTouches[p269] == nil then
              		p268.fingerTouches[p269] = p270
              		if not p270 then
              			p268.numUnsunkTouches = p268.numUnsunkTouches + 1
              		end
              	end
              end
              function v_u_248.OnTouchEnded(p271, p272, _) -- name: OnTouchEnded
              	if p271.fingerTouches[p272] ~= nil and p271.fingerTouches[p272] == false then
              		p271.numUnsunkTouches = p271.numUnsunkTouches - 1
              	end
              	p271.fingerTouches[p272] = nil
              end
              function v_u_248.OnPreferredInputChanged(_) -- name: OnPreferredInputChanged
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_24, (copy) v_u_7
              	local v273 = v_u_24.Character
              	if v273 then
              		local v274 = v_u_7.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch
              		for _, v275 in pairs(v273:GetChildren()) do
              			if v275:IsA("Tool") then
              				v275.ManualActivationOnly = v274
              			end
              		end
              	end
              end
              function v_u_248.OnCharacterAdded(p_u_276, p277) -- name: OnCharacterAdded
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_7, (copy) v_u_23, (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201, (copy) v_u_25, (copy) v_u_13, (copy) v_u_17
              	p_u_276:DisconnectEvents()
              	p_u_276.inputBeganConn = v_u_7.InputBegan:Connect(function(p278, p279)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_276, (ref) v_u_23, (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201, (ref) v_u_25
              		if p278.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
              			p_u_276:OnTouchBegan(p278, p279)
              		end
              		if p_u_276.wasdEnabled and (p279 == false and (p278.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and v_u_23[p278.KeyCode])) then
              			if v_u_199 then
              				v_u_199:Cancel()
              				v_u_199 = nil
              			end
              			if v_u_200 then
              				v_u_200:Disconnect()
              				v_u_200 = nil
              			end
              			if v_u_201 then
              				v_u_201:Disconnect()
              				v_u_201 = nil
              			end
              			v_u_25.CancelFailureAnimation()
              		end
              		if p278.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
              			p_u_276.mouse2DownTime = tick()
              			p_u_276.mouse2DownPos = p278.Position
              		end
              	end)
              	p_u_276.inputChangedConn = v_u_7.InputChanged:Connect(function(p280, p281)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_276
              		if p280.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
              			p_u_276:OnTouchChanged(p280, p281)
              		end
              	end)
              	p_u_276.inputEndedConn = v_u_7.InputEnded:Connect(function(p282, p283)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_276, (ref) v_u_199
              		if p282.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
              			p_u_276:OnTouchEnded(p282, p283)
              		end
              		if p282.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
              			p_u_276.mouse2UpTime = tick()
              			local v284 = p282.Position
              			local v285 = v_u_199 or p_u_276.keyboardMoveVector.Magnitude <= 0
              			if p_u_276.mouse2UpTime - p_u_276.mouse2DownTime < 0.25 and ((v284 - p_u_276.mouse2DownPos).magnitude < 5 and v285) then
              				OnTap({ v284 })
              			end
              		end
              	end)
              	p_u_276.tapConn = v_u_7.TouchTap:Connect(function(p286, p287)
              		if not p287 then
              			OnTap(p286, nil, true)
              		end
              	end)
              	p_u_276.menuOpenedConnection = v_u_13.MenuOpened:Connect(function()
              		-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201
              		if v_u_199 then
              			v_u_199:Cancel()
              			v_u_199 = nil
              		end
              		if v_u_200 then
              			v_u_200:Disconnect()
              			v_u_200 = nil
              		end
              		if v_u_201 then
              			v_u_201:Disconnect()
              			v_u_201 = nil
              		end
              	end)
              	local function v_u_291(p288) -- name: OnCharacterChildAdded
              		-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_17, (ref) v_u_7, (copy) p_u_276
              		local v289
              		if v_u_17 then
              			v289 = v_u_7.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch
              		else
              			v289 = v_u_7.TouchEnabled
              		end
              		if v289 and p288:IsA("Tool") then
              			p288.ManualActivationOnly = true
              		end
              		if p288:IsA("Humanoid") then
              			local v290 = p_u_276.humanoidDiedConn
              			if v290 then
              				v290:Disconnect()
              			end
              			p_u_276.humanoidDiedConn = p288.Died:Connect(function() end)
              		end
              	end
              	p_u_276.characterChildAddedConn = p277.ChildAdded:Connect(function(p292)
              		-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_291
              		v_u_291(p292)
              	end)
              	p_u_276.characterChildRemovedConn = p277.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(p293)
              		-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_17, (ref) v_u_7
              		local v294
              		if v_u_17 then
              			v294 = v_u_7.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch
              		else
              			v294 = v_u_7.TouchEnabled
              		end
              		if v294 and p293:IsA("Tool") then
              			p293.ManualActivationOnly = false
              		end
              	end)
              	for _, v295 in pairs(p277:GetChildren()) do
              		v_u_291(v295)
              	end
              	if v_u_17 then
              		p_u_276.preferredInputChangedConnection = v_u_7:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PreferredInput"):Connect(function()
              			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_276
              			p_u_276:OnPreferredInputChanged()
              		end)
              	end
              end
              function v_u_248.Start(p296) -- name: Start
              	p296:Enable(true)
              end
              function v_u_248.Stop(p297) -- name: Stop
              	p297:Enable(false)
              end
              function v_u_248.CleanupPath(_) -- name: CleanupPath
              	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201
              	if v_u_199 then
              		v_u_199:Cancel()
              		v_u_199 = nil
              	end
              	if v_u_200 then
              		v_u_200:Disconnect()
              		v_u_200 = nil
              	end
              	if v_u_201 then
              		v_u_201:Disconnect()
              		v_u_201 = nil
              	end
              end
              function v_u_248.Enable(p_u_298, p299, p300, p301) -- name: Enable
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_24, (ref) v_u_199, (ref) v_u_200, (ref) v_u_201, (copy) v_u_17, (copy) v_u_7, (copy) v_u_247
              	if p299 then
              		if not p_u_298.running then
              			if v_u_24.Character then
              				p_u_298:OnCharacterAdded(v_u_24.Character)
              			end
              			p_u_298.onCharacterAddedConn = v_u_24.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(p302)
              				-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_298
              				p_u_298:OnCharacterAdded(p302)
              			end)
              			p_u_298.running = true
              		end
              		p_u_298.touchJumpController = p301
              		if p_u_298.touchJumpController then
              			p_u_298.touchJumpController:Enable(p_u_298.jumpEnabled)
              		end
              	else
              		if p_u_298.running then
              			p_u_298:DisconnectEvents()
              			if v_u_199 then
              				v_u_199:Cancel()
              				v_u_199 = nil
              			end
              			if v_u_200 then
              				v_u_200:Disconnect()
              				v_u_200 = nil
              			end
              			if v_u_201 then
              				v_u_201:Disconnect()
              				v_u_201 = nil
              			end
              			local v303
              			if v_u_17 then
              				v303 = v_u_7.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch
              			else
              				v303 = v_u_7.TouchEnabled
              			end
              			if v303 then
              				local v304 = v_u_24.Character
              				if v304 then
              					for _, v305 in pairs(v304:GetChildren()) do
              						if v305:IsA("Tool") then
              							v305.ManualActivationOnly = false
              						end
              					end
              				end
              			end
              			p_u_298.running = false
              		end
              		if p_u_298.touchJumpController and not p_u_298.jumpEnabled then
              			p_u_298.touchJumpController:Enable(true)
              		end
              		p_u_298.touchJumpController = nil
              	end
              	v_u_247.Enable(p_u_298, p299)
              	p_u_298.wasdEnabled = p299 and p300 and p300 or false
              	p_u_298.enabled = p299
              end
              function v_u_248.OnRenderStepped(p306, p307) -- name: OnRenderStepped
              	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_199
              	p306.isJumping = false
              	if v_u_199 then
              		v_u_199:OnRenderStepped(p307)
              		if v_u_199 then
              			p306.moveVector = v_u_199.NextActionMoveDirection
              			p306.moveVectorIsCameraRelative = false
              			if v_u_199.NextActionJump then
              				p306.isJumping = true
              			end
              		else
              			p306.moveVector = p306.keyboardMoveVector
              			p306.moveVectorIsCameraRelative = true

              	end
              	if p306.jumpRequested then
              		p306.isJumping = true
              	end
              end

              		local v311 = p308.forwardValue + p308.backwardValue
              		p308.keyboardMoveVector = Vector3.new(v310, 0, v311)
              	end
              end
