              function v_u_17.ShouldUseNavigationLaser(p26) -- name: ShouldUseNavigationLaser
              	if p26:IsMobileVR() then
              		return true
              	elseif p26:HasBothHandControllers() then
              		return false
              	else
              		return p26:HasAnyHandControllers() and true or not p26:HasGamepad()
              	end
              end
              function v_u_17.StartFollowingPath(p27, p28) -- name: StartFollowingPath
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_14
              	currentPath = p28
              	currentPoints = currentPath:GetPointCoordinates()
              	currentPointIdx = 1
              	moving = true
              	timeReachedLastPoint = tick()
              	local v29 = p27:GetLocalHumanoid()
              	if v29 and (v29.Torso and #currentPoints >= 1) then
              		expectedTimeToNextPoint = (currentPoints[1] - v29.Torso.Position).magnitude / v29.WalkSpeed
              	end
              	v_u_14:Fire("targetPoint", p27.currentDestination)
              end
              function v_u_17.GoToPoint(p30, p31) -- name: GoToPoint
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_14
              	currentPath = true
              	currentPoints = { p31 }
              	currentPointIdx = 1
              	moving = true
              	local v32 = p30:GetLocalHumanoid()
              	local v33 = (v32.Torso.Position - p31).magnitude / v32.WalkSpeed
              	timeReachedLastPoint = tick()
              	expectedTimeToNextPoint = v33
              	v_u_14:Fire("targetPoint", p31)
              end
              function v_u_17.StopFollowingPath(p34) -- name: StopFollowingPath
              	currentPath = nil
              	currentPoints = nil
              	currentPointIdx = 0
              	moving = false
              	p34.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              end
              function v_u_17.TryComputePath(_, p35, p36) -- name: TryComputePath
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_5
              	local v37 = nil
              	local v38 = 0
              	while not v37 and v38 < 5 do
              		v37 = v_u_5:ComputeSmoothPathAsync(p35, p36, 200)
              		v38 = v38 + 1
              		if v37.Status == Enum.PathStatus.ClosestNoPath or v37.Status == Enum.PathStatus.ClosestOutOfRange then
              			return nil
              		end
              		if v37 and v37.Status == Enum.PathStatus.FailStartNotEmpty then
              			p35 = p35 + (p36 - p35).Unit
              			v37 = nil
              		end
              		if v37 and v37.Status == Enum.PathStatus.FailFinishNotEmpty then
              			p36 = p36 + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
              			v37 = nil
              		end
              	end
              	return v37
              end
              function v_u_17.OnNavigationRequest(p39, p40, _) -- name: OnNavigationRequest
              	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_10
              	local v41 = p40.Position
              	local v42 = p39.currentDestination
              	local v43 = v41.x
              	local v44
              	if v43 == v43 and v43 ~= (1 / 0) then
              		v44 = v43 ~= (-1 / 0)
              	else
              		v44 = false
              	end
              	if v44 then
              		local v45 = v41.y
              		if v45 == v45 and v45 ~= (1 / 0) then
              			v44 = v45 ~= (-1 / 0)
              		else
              			v44 = false
              		end
              		if v44 then
              			local v46 = v41.z
              			if v46 == v46 and v46 ~= (1 / 0) then
              				v44 = v46 ~= (-1 / 0)
              			else
              				v44 = false
              			end
              		end
              	end
              	if v44 then
              		p39.currentDestination = v41
              		local v47 = p39:GetLocalHumanoid()
              		if v47 and v47.Torso then
              			local v48 = v47.Torso.Position
              			if (p39.currentDestination - v48).magnitude < 12 then
              				p39:GoToPoint(p39.currentDestination)
              			elseif v42 and (p39.currentDestination - v42).magnitude <= 4 then
              				if moving then
              					p39.currentPoints[#currentPoints] = p39.currentDestination
              					return
              				end
              				p39:GoToPoint(p39.currentDestination)
              			else
              				local v49 = p39:TryComputePath(v48, p39.currentDestination)
              				if v49 then
              					p39:StartFollowingPath(v49)
              					if v_u_10 then
              						v_u_10.setCurrentPoints(p39.currentPoints)
              						v_u_10.renderPath()
              						return
              					end
              				else
              					p39:StopFollowingPath()
              					if v_u_10 then
              						v_u_10.clearRenderedPath()
              						return
              					end
              				end
              			end
              		else
              			return
              		end
              	else
              		return
              	end
              end
              function v_u_17.OnJumpAction(p50, _, p51, _) -- name: OnJumpAction
              	if p51 == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
              		p50.isJumping = true
              	end
              	return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
              end
              function v_u_17.BindJumpAction(p_u_52, p53) -- name: BindJumpAction
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_6
              	if p53 then
              		if not p_u_52.isJumpBound then
              			p_u_52.isJumpBound = true
              			v_u_6:BindActionAtPriority("VRJumpAction", function()
              				-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_52
              				return p_u_52:OnJumpAction()
              			end, false, p_u_52.CONTROL_ACTION_PRIORITY, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)
              			return
              		end
              	elseif p_u_52.isJumpBound then
              		p_u_52.isJumpBound = false
              		v_u_6:UnbindAction("VRJumpAction")
              	end
              end
              function v_u_17.ControlCharacterGamepad(p54, _, p55, p56) -- name: ControlCharacterGamepad
              	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_10, (copy) v_u_14
              	if p56.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 then
              		if p55 ~= Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
              			if p55 == Enum.UserInputState.End then
              				p54.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              				if p54:ShouldUseNavigationLaser() then
              					p54:BindJumpAction(false)
              					p54:SetLaserPointerMode("Navigation")
              				end
              				if p54.moveLatch then
              					p54.moveLatch = false
              					v_u_14:Fire("offtrack")
              				end
              			else
              				p54:StopFollowingPath()
              				if v_u_10 then
              					v_u_10.clearRenderedPath()
              				end
              				if p54:ShouldUseNavigationLaser() then
              					p54:BindJumpAction(true)
              					p54:SetLaserPointerMode("Hidden")
              				end
              				if p56.Position.magnitude > 0.22 then
              					local v57 = p56.Position.X
              					local v58 = -p56.Position.Y
              					p54.moveVector = Vector3.new(v57, 0, v58)
              					if p54.moveVector.magnitude > 0 then
              						local v59 = p54.moveVector.unit
              						local v60 = p56.Position.magnitude
              						p54.moveVector = v59 * math.min(1, v60)
              					end
              					p54.moveLatch = true
              				end
              			end
              			return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
              		end
              		p54.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              	end
              end
              function v_u_17.OnHeartbeat(p61, p62) -- name: OnHeartbeat
              	-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_10, (copy) v_u_13, (copy) v_u_12, (copy) v_u_14
              	local v63 = p61.moveVector
              	local v64 = p61:GetLocalHumanoid()
              	if v64 and v64.Torso then
              		if p61.moving and p61.currentPoints then
              			local v65 = v64.Torso.Position
              			local v66 = (currentPoints[1] - v65) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
              			local v67 = v66.magnitude
              			local v68 = v66 / v67
              			if v67 < 1 then
              				local v69 = currentPoints[1]
              				local v70 = 0
              				for v71, v72 in pairs(currentPoints) do
              					if v71 ~= 1 then
              						v70 = v70 + (v72 - v69).magnitude / v64.WalkSpeed
              						v69 = v72
              					end
              				end
              				table.remove(currentPoints, 1)
              				currentPointIdx = currentPointIdx + 1
              				if #currentPoints == 0 then
              					p61:StopFollowingPath()
              					if v_u_10 then
              						v_u_10.clearRenderedPath()
              					end
              					return
              				end
              				if v_u_10 then
              					v_u_10.setCurrentPoints(currentPoints)
              					v_u_10.renderPath()
              				end
              				expectedTimeToNextPoint = (currentPoints[1] - v65).magnitude / v64.WalkSpeed
              				timeReachedLastPoint = tick()
              			else
              				if v_u_13 then
              					v_u_12.FilterDescendantsInstances = { game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character, workspace.CurrentCamera }
              					local v73 = workspace:Raycast(v65 - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), v68 * 3, v_u_12)
              					if v73 then
              						local v74 = workspace:Raycast(v73.Position + v68 * 0.5 + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0), Vector3.new(-0, -100, -0), v_u_12).Position.Y - v65.Y
              						if v74 < 6 and v74 > -2 then
              							v64.Jump = true
              						end
              					end
              				else
              					local v75 = { game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character, workspace.CurrentCamera }
              					local v76 = Ray.new(v65 - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), v68 * 3)
              					local v77, v78, _ = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(v76, v75)
              					if v77 then
              						local v79 = Ray.new(v78 + v68 * 0.5 + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0), Vector3.new(-0, -100, -0))
              						local _, v80, _ = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(v79, v75)
              						local v81 = v80.Y - v65.Y
              						if v81 < 6 and v81 > -2 then
              							v64.Jump = true
              						end
              					end
              				end
              				if tick() - timeReachedLastPoint > expectedTimeToNextPoint + 2 then
              					p61:StopFollowingPath()
              					if v_u_10 then
              						v_u_10.clearRenderedPath()
              					end
              					v_u_14:Fire("offtrack")
              				end
              				v63 = p61.moveVector:Lerp(v68, p62 * 10)
              			end
              		end
              		local v82 = v63.x
              		local v83
              		if v82 == v82 and v82 ~= (1 / 0) then
              			v83 = v82 ~= (-1 / 0)
              		else
              			v83 = false
              		end
              		if v83 then
              			local v84 = v63.y
              			if v84 == v84 and v84 ~= (1 / 0) then
              				v83 = v84 ~= (-1 / 0)
              			else
              				v83 = false
              			end
              			if v83 then
              				local v85 = v63.z
              				if v85 == v85 and v85 ~= (1 / 0) then
              					v83 = v85 ~= (-1 / 0)
              				else
              					v83 = false
              				end
              			end
              		end
              		if v83 then
              			p61.moveVector = v63
              		end
              	end
              end
              function v_u_17.OnUserCFrameEnabled(p86) -- name: OnUserCFrameEnabled
              	if p86:ShouldUseNavigationLaser() then
              		p86:BindJumpAction(false)
              		p86:SetLaserPointerMode("Navigation")
              	else
              		p86:BindJumpAction(true)
              		p86:SetLaserPointerMode("Hidden")
              	end
              end
              function v_u_17.Enable(p_u_87, p88) -- name: Enable
              	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1, (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_6
              	p_u_87.moveVector = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
              	p_u_87.isJumping = false
              	if p88 then
              		p_u_87.navigationRequestedConn = v_u_1.NavigationRequested:Connect(function(p89, p90)
              			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_87
              			p_u_87:OnNavigationRequest(p89, p90)
              		end)
              		p_u_87.heartbeatConn = v_u_3.Heartbeat:Connect(function(p91)
              			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_87
              			p_u_87:OnHeartbeat(p91)
              		end)
              		v_u_6:BindAction("MoveThumbstick", function(p92, p93, p94)
              			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_87
              			return p_u_87:ControlCharacterGamepad(p92, p93, p94)
              		end, false, p_u_87.CONTROL_ACTION_PRIORITY, Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1)
              		v_u_6:BindActivate(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2)
              		p_u_87.userCFrameEnabledConn = v_u_1.UserCFrameEnabled:Connect(function()
              			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_87
              			p_u_87:OnUserCFrameEnabled()
              		end)
              		p_u_87:OnUserCFrameEnabled()
              		v_u_1:SetTouchpadMode(Enum.VRTouchpad.Left, Enum.VRTouchpadMode.VirtualThumbstick)
              		v_u_1:SetTouchpadMode(Enum.VRTouchpad.Right, Enum.VRTouchpadMode.ABXY)
              		p_u_87.enabled = true
              	else
              		p_u_87:StopFollowingPath()
              		v_u_6:UnbindAction("MoveThumbstick")
              		v_u_6:UnbindActivate(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2)
              		p_u_87:BindJumpAction(false)
              		p_u_87:SetLaserPointerMode("Disabled")
              		if p_u_87.navigationRequestedConn then
              			p_u_87.navigationRequestedConn:Disconnect()
              			p_u_87.navigationRequestedConn = nil
              		end
              		if p_u_87.heartbeatConn then
              			p_u_87.heartbeatConn:Disconnect()
              			p_u_87.heartbeatConn = nil
              		end
              		if p_u_87.userCFrameEnabledConn then
              			p_u_87.userCFrameEnabledConn:Disconnect()
              			p_u_87.userCFrameEnabledConn = nil
              		end
              		p_u_87.enabled = false
              	end
              end
              return v_u_17

          - [ModuleScript] ClickToMoveDisplay
            --- [СКОПИРОВАННЫЙ КОД СРИПТА] ---
              local v1 = {}
              local v_u_2 = "rbxasset://textures/ui/traildot.png"

            assert(v26, "Strict typing check")
            local v27 = v_u_4.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts")
            v27:RegisterTouchCameraMovementMode(Enum.TouchCameraMovementMode.Default)
            v27:RegisterTouchCameraMovementMode(Enum.TouchCameraMovementMode.Follow)
            v27:RegisterTouchCameraMovementMode(Enum.TouchCameraMovementMode.Classic)
            v27:RegisterComputerCameraMovementMode(Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Default)
            v27:RegisterComputerCameraMovementMode(Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow)
            v27:RegisterComputerCameraMovementMode(Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic)
            v27:RegisterComputerCameraMovementMode(Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.CameraToggle)
            local v_u_28 = v11.getUserFlag("UserRespectLegacyCameraOptions")
            local v_u_29 = v11.getUserFlag("UserPlayerConnectionMemoryLeak")
            local v_u_30 = v11.getUserFlag("UserPreferredInputPlayerScripts2")
            function v_u_1.new() -- name: new
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_22, (copy) v_u_29, (copy) v_u_10, (copy) v_u_1, (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_6, (copy) v_u_30, (copy) v_u_23, (copy) v_u_28, (copy) v_u_5, (copy) v_u_2, (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_8
            	local v31 = {
            		["activeTransparencyController"] = v_u_22.new()
            	}
            	local v32
            	if v_u_29 then
            		v32 = v_u_10.new()
            	else
            		v32 = nil
            	end
            	v31.connectionUtil = v32
            	local v33 = v_u_1
            	local v_u_34 = setmetatable(v31, v33)
            	v_u_34.activeCameraController = nil
            	v_u_34.activeOcclusionModule = nil
            	v_u_34.activeMouseLockController = nil
            	v_u_34.currentComputerCameraMovementMode = nil
            	v_u_34.cameraSubjectChangedConn = nil
            	v_u_34.cameraTypeChangedConn = nil
            	for _, v35 in pairs(v_u_4:GetPlayers()) do
            		v_u_34:OnPlayerAdded(v35)
            	end
            	v_u_4.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p36)
            		-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_34
            		v_u_34:OnPlayerAdded(p36)
            	end)
            	if v_u_29 then
            		v_u_4.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p37)
            			-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_34
            			v_u_34:OnPlayerRemoving(p37)
            		end)
            	end
            	v_u_34.activeTransparencyController:Enable(true)
            	if not v_u_6.TouchEnabled or v_u_30 then
            		v_u_34.activeMouseLockController = v_u_23.new()
            		local v38 = v_u_34.activeMouseLockController
            		assert(v38, "Strict typing check")
            		local v39 = v_u_34.activeMouseLockController:GetBindableToggleEvent()
            		if v39 then
            			v39:Connect(function()
            				-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_34
            				v_u_34:OnMouseLockToggled()
            			end)
            		end
            	end
            	if v_u_28 then
            		v_u_34:ActivateCameraController()
            	else
            		v_u_34:ActivateCameraController(v_u_34:GetCameraControlChoice())
            	end
            	v_u_34:ActivateOcclusionModule(v_u_4.LocalPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode)
            	v_u_34:OnCurrentCameraChanged()
            	v_u_5:BindToRenderStep("cameraRenderUpdate", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value, function(p40)
            		-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_34
            		v_u_34:Update(p40)
            	end)
            	for _, v_u_41 in pairs(v_u_2) do
            		v_u_4.LocalPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal(v_u_41):Connect(function()
            			-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_34, (copy) v_u_41
            			v_u_34:OnLocalPlayerCameraPropertyChanged(v_u_41)
            		end)
            	end
            	for _, v_u_42 in pairs(v_u_3) do
            		v_u_8:GetPropertyChangedSignal(v_u_42):Connect(function()
            			-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_34, (copy) v_u_42
            			v_u_34:OnUserGameSettingsPropertyChanged(v_u_42)
            		end)
            	end
            	game.Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
            		-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_34
            		v_u_34:OnCurrentCameraChanged()
            	end)
            	if v_u_30 then
            		v_u_6:GetPropertyChangedSignal("PreferredInput"):Connect(function()
            			-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_34
            			v_u_34:OnPreferredInputChanged()
            		end)
            	end
            	return v_u_34
            end
            function v_u_1.GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings(_) -- name: GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_12, (copy) v_u_30, (copy) v_u_6, (copy) v_u_8
            	if v_u_4.LocalPlayer.CameraMode == Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson then
            		return v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic)
            	else
            		local v43
            		if v_u_30 then
            			v43 = v_u_6.PreferredInput == Enum.PreferredInput.Touch
            		else
            			v43 = v_u_6.TouchEnabled
            		end
            		local v44, v45
            		if v43 then
            			v44 = v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v_u_4.LocalPlayer.DevTouchCameraMode)
            			v45 = v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v_u_8.TouchCameraMovementMode)
            		else
            			v44 = v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v_u_4.LocalPlayer.DevComputerCameraMode)
            			v45 = v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v_u_8.ComputerCameraMovementMode)
            		end
            		if v44 == Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.UserChoice then
            			return v45
            		else
            			return v44
            		end
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.ActivateOcclusionModule(p46, p47) -- name: ActivateOcclusionModule
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_21, (copy) v_u_20, (copy) v_u_25, (copy) v_u_4
            	local v48
            	if p47 == Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Zoom then
            		v48 = v_u_21
            	else
            		if p47 ~= Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam then
            			warn("CameraScript ActivateOcclusionModule called with unsupported mode")
            			return
            		end
            		v48 = v_u_20
            	end
            	p46.occlusionMode = p47
            	if p46.activeOcclusionModule and p46.activeOcclusionModule:GetOcclusionMode() == p47 then
            		if not p46.activeOcclusionModule:GetEnabled() then
            			p46.activeOcclusionModule:Enable(true)
            		end
            	else
            		local v49 = p46.activeOcclusionModule
            		p46.activeOcclusionModule = v_u_25[v48]
            		if not p46.activeOcclusionModule then
            			p46.activeOcclusionModule = v48.new()
            			if p46.activeOcclusionModule then
            				v_u_25[v48] = p46.activeOcclusionModule
            			end
            		end
            		if p46.activeOcclusionModule then
            			if p46.activeOcclusionModule:GetOcclusionMode() ~= p47 then
            				warn("CameraScript ActivateOcclusionModule mismatch: ", p46.activeOcclusionModule:GetOcclusionMode(), "~=", p47)
            			end
            			if v49 then
            				if v49 == p46.activeOcclusionModule then
            					warn("CameraScript ActivateOcclusionModule failure to detect already running correct module")
            				else
            					v49:Enable(false)
            				end
            			end
            			if p47 == Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam then
            				if v_u_4.LocalPlayer.Character then
            					p46.activeOcclusionModule:CharacterAdded(v_u_4.LocalPlayer.Character, v_u_4.LocalPlayer)
            				end
            			else
            				for _, v50 in pairs(v_u_4:GetPlayers()) do
            					if v50 and v50.Character then
            						p46.activeOcclusionModule:CharacterAdded(v50.Character, v50)
            					end
            				end
            				p46.activeOcclusionModule:OnCameraSubjectChanged(game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject)
            			end
            			p46.activeOcclusionModule:Enable(true)
            		end
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.ShouldUseVehicleCamera(p51) -- name: ShouldUseVehicleCamera
            	local v52 = workspace.CurrentCamera
            	if not v52 then
            		return false
            	end
            	local v53 = v52.CameraType
            	local v54 = v52.CameraSubject
            	local v55 = v53 == Enum.CameraType.Custom and true or v53 == Enum.CameraType.Follow
            	local v56 = v54 and v54:IsA("VehicleSeat") or false
            	local v57 = p51.occlusionMode ~= Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam
            	if v56 then
            		if not v55 then
            			v57 = v55
            		end
            	else
            		v57 = v56
            	end
            	return v57
            end
            function v_u_1.ActivateCameraController(p58, p59, p60) -- name: ActivateCameraController
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_28, (copy) v_u_16, (copy) v_u_7, (copy) v_u_18, (copy) v_u_14, (copy) v_u_15, (copy) v_u_19, (copy) v_u_17, (copy) v_u_24
            	if v_u_28 then
            		p60 = workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType
            		p59 = p58:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings()
            	end
            	local v61 = nil
            	if v_u_28 and true or p60 ~= nil then
            		if p60 == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
            			if p58.activeCameraController then
            				p58.activeCameraController:Enable(false)
            				p58.activeCameraController = nil
            			end
            			return
            		end
            		if p60 == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
            			p59 = p58:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings()
            		elseif p60 == Enum.CameraType.Track then
            			p59 = Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
            		elseif p60 == Enum.CameraType.Follow then
            			p59 = Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow
            		elseif p60 == Enum.CameraType.Orbital then
            			p59 = Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Orbital
            		elseif p60 == Enum.CameraType.Attach or (p60 == Enum.CameraType.Watch or p60 == Enum.CameraType.Fixed) then
            			v61 = v_u_16
            		else
            			warn("CameraScript encountered an unhandled Camera.CameraType value: ", p60)
            		end
            	end
            	if not v61 then
            		if v_u_7.VREnabled then
            			v61 = v_u_18
            		elseif p59 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic or (p59 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow or (p59 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Default or p59 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.CameraToggle)) then
            			v61 = v_u_14
            		else
            			if p59 ~= Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Orbital then
            				warn("ActivateCameraController did not select a module.")
            				return
            			end
            			v61 = v_u_15
            		end
            	end
            	if p58:ShouldUseVehicleCamera() then
            		if v_u_7.VREnabled then
            			v61 = v_u_19
            		else
            			v61 = v_u_17
            		end
            	end
            	local v62
            	if v_u_24[v61] then
            		v62 = v_u_24[v61]
            		if v62.Reset then
            			v62:Reset()
            		end
            	else
            		v62 = v61.new()
            		v_u_24[v61] = v62
            	end
            	if p58.activeCameraController then
            		if p58.activeCameraController == v62 then
            			if not p58.activeCameraController:GetEnabled() then
            				p58.activeCameraController:Enable(true)
            			end
            		else
            			p58.activeCameraController:Enable(false)
            			p58.activeCameraController = v62
            			p58.activeCameraController:Enable(true)
            		end
            	elseif v62 ~= nil then
            		p58.activeCameraController = v62
            		local v63 = p58.activeCameraController
            		assert(v63, "Strict typing check")
            		p58.activeCameraController:Enable(true)
            	end
            	if p58.activeCameraController then
            		if v_u_28 then
            			p58.activeCameraController:SetCameraMovementMode(p59)
            			p58.activeCameraController:SetCameraType(p60)
            			return
            		end
            		if p59 ~= nil then
            			p58.activeCameraController:SetCameraMovementMode(p59)
            			return
            		end
            		if p60 ~= nil then
            			p58.activeCameraController:SetCameraType(p60)
            		end
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnCameraSubjectChanged(p64) -- name: OnCameraSubjectChanged
            	local v65 = workspace.CurrentCamera
            	local v66
            	if v65 then
            		v66 = v65.CameraSubject
            	else
            		v66 = nil
            	end
            	if p64.activeTransparencyController then
            		p64.activeTransparencyController:SetSubject(v66)
            	end
            	if p64.activeOcclusionModule then
            		p64.activeOcclusionModule:OnCameraSubjectChanged(v66)
            	end
            	local v67 = nil
            	local v68
            	if v65 then
            		v68 = v65.CameraType
            	else
            		v68 = nil
            	end
            	p64:ActivateCameraController(v67, v68)
            end
            function v_u_1.OnCameraTypeChanged(p69, p70) -- name: OnCameraTypeChanged
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_6, (copy) v_u_12
            	if p70 == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable and v_u_6.MouseBehavior == Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then
            		v_u_12.restoreMouseBehavior()
            	end
            	p69:ActivateCameraController(nil, p70)
            end
            function v_u_1.OnCurrentCameraChanged(p_u_71) -- name: OnCurrentCameraChanged
            	local v_u_72 = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera
            	if v_u_72 then
            		if p_u_71.cameraSubjectChangedConn then
            			p_u_71.cameraSubjectChangedConn:Disconnect()
            		end
            		if p_u_71.cameraTypeChangedConn then
            			p_u_71.cameraTypeChangedConn:Disconnect()
            		end
            		p_u_71.cameraSubjectChangedConn = v_u_72:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraSubject"):Connect(function()
            			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_71
            			p_u_71:OnCameraSubjectChanged()
            		end)
            		p_u_71.cameraTypeChangedConn = v_u_72:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraType"):Connect(function()
            			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_71, (copy) v_u_72
            			p_u_71:OnCameraTypeChanged(v_u_72.CameraType)
            		end)
            		p_u_71:OnCameraSubjectChanged()
            		p_u_71:OnCameraTypeChanged(v_u_72.CameraType)
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnLocalPlayerCameraPropertyChanged(p73, p74) -- name: OnLocalPlayerCameraPropertyChanged
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_12
            	if p74 == "CameraMode" then
            		if v_u_4.LocalPlayer.CameraMode ~= Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson then
            			if v_u_4.LocalPlayer.CameraMode == Enum.CameraMode.Classic then
            				local v75 = p73:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings()
            				p73:ActivateCameraController(v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v75))
            			else
            				warn("Unhandled value for property player.CameraMode: ", v_u_4.LocalPlayer.CameraMode)
            			end
            		end
            		if not p73.activeCameraController or p73.activeCameraController:GetModuleName() ~= "ClassicCamera" then
            			p73:ActivateCameraController(v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic))
            		end
            		if p73.activeCameraController then
            			p73.activeCameraController:UpdateForDistancePropertyChange()
            			return
            		end
            	else
            		if p74 == "DevComputerCameraMode" or p74 == "DevTouchCameraMode" then
            			local v76 = p73:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings()
            			p73:ActivateCameraController(v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v76))
            			return
            		end
            		if p74 == "DevCameraOcclusionMode" then
            			p73:ActivateOcclusionModule(v_u_4.LocalPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode)
            			return
            		end
            		if p74 == "CameraMinZoomDistance" or p74 == "CameraMaxZoomDistance" then
            			if p73.activeCameraController then
            				p73.activeCameraController:UpdateForDistancePropertyChange()
            				return
            			end
            		else
            			if p74 == "DevTouchMovementMode" then
            				return
            			end
            			if p74 == "DevComputerMovementMode" then
            				return
            			end
            			local _ = p74 == "DevEnableMouseLock"
            		end
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnUserGameSettingsPropertyChanged(p77, p78) -- name: OnUserGameSettingsPropertyChanged
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_12
            	if p78 == "ComputerCameraMovementMode" then
            		local v79 = p77:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings()
            		p77:ActivateCameraController(v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v79))
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnPreferredInputChanged(p80) -- name: OnPreferredInputChanged
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_12
            	local v81 = p80:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings()
            	p80:ActivateCameraController(v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v81))
            end
            function v_u_1.Update(p82, p83) -- name: Update
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_13
            	if p82.activeCameraController then
            		p82.activeCameraController:UpdateMouseBehavior()
            		local v84, v85 = p82.activeCameraController:Update(p83)
            		if p82.activeOcclusionModule then
            			v84, v85 = p82.activeOcclusionModule:Update(p83, v84, v85)
            		end
            		local v86 = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera
            		v86.CFrame = v84
            		v86.Focus = v85
            		if p82.activeTransparencyController then
            			p82.activeTransparencyController:Update(p83)
            		end
            		if v_u_13.getInputEnabled() then
            			v_u_13.resetInputForFrameEnd()
            		end
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.GetCameraControlChoice(_) -- name: GetCameraControlChoice
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_28, (copy) v_u_6, (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_12, (copy) v_u_8
            	local v87 = not v_u_28
            	assert(v87, "CameraModule:GetCameraControlChoice should not be called when FFlagUserRespectLegacyCameraOptions is enabled")
            	if v_u_6:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or v_u_6.TouchEnabled then
            		if v_u_4.LocalPlayer.DevTouchCameraMode == Enum.DevTouchCameraMovementMode.UserChoice then
            			return v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v_u_8.TouchCameraMovementMode)
            		else
            			return v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v_u_4.LocalPlayer.DevTouchCameraMode)
            		end
            	else
            		if v_u_4.LocalPlayer.DevComputerCameraMode ~= Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.UserChoice then
            			return v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v_u_4.LocalPlayer.DevComputerCameraMode)
            		end
            		local v88 = v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v_u_8.ComputerCameraMovementMode)
            		return v_u_12.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(v88)
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnCharacterAdded(p89, p90, p91) -- name: OnCharacterAdded
            	if p89.activeOcclusionModule then
            		p89.activeOcclusionModule:CharacterAdded(p90, p91)
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnCharacterRemoving(p92, p93, p94) -- name: OnCharacterRemoving
            	if p92.activeOcclusionModule then
            		p92.activeOcclusionModule:CharacterRemoving(p93, p94)
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnPlayerAdded(p_u_95, p_u_96) -- name: OnPlayerAdded
            	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_29
            	if v_u_29 then
            		if p_u_95.connectionUtil then
            			p_u_95.connectionUtil:trackConnection(("%*CharacterAdded"):format(p_u_96.UserId), p_u_96.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(p97)
            				-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_95, (copy) p_u_96
            				p_u_95:OnCharacterAdded(p97, p_u_96)
            			end))
            			p_u_95.connectionUtil:trackConnection(("%*CharacterRemoving"):format(p_u_96.UserId), p_u_96.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(p98)
            				-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_95, (copy) p_u_96
            				p_u_95:OnCharacterRemoving(p98, p_u_96)
            			end))
            			return
            		end
            	else
            		p_u_96.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(p99)
            			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_95, (copy) p_u_96
            			p_u_95:OnCharacterAdded(p99, p_u_96)
            		end)
            		p_u_96.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(p100)
            			-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_95, (copy) p_u_96
            			p_u_95:OnCharacterRemoving(p100, p_u_96)
            		end)
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnPlayerRemoving(p101, p102) -- name: OnPlayerRemoving
            	if p101.connectionUtil then
            		p101.connectionUtil:disconnect((("%*CharacterAdded"):format(p102.UserId)))
            		p101.connectionUtil:disconnect((("%*CharacterRemoving"):format(p102.UserId)))
            	end
            end
            function v_u_1.OnMouseLockToggled(p103) -- name: OnMouseLockToggled
            	if p103.activeMouseLockController then
            		local v104 = p103.activeMouseLockController:GetIsMouseLocked()
            		local v105 = p103.activeMouseLockController:GetMouseLockOffset()
            		if p103.activeCameraController then
            			p103.activeCameraController:SetIsMouseLocked(v104)
            			p103.activeCameraController:SetMouseLockOffset(v105)
            		end
            	end
            end
            v_u_1.new()
            return {}

        - [Folder] CommonUtils
      - [LocalScript] CameraManager
        --- [СКОПИРОВАННЫЙ КОД СРИПТА] ---
          local v_u_1 = game:GetService("UserInputService")
          local v2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts")
          local v_u_3 = require(v2:WaitForChild("Data"))
          local v_u_4 = workspace.CurrentCamera
          local v5 = require(game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("SignalManager"))
          local v6 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts")
          local v_u_7 = require(v6.PlayerModule.CameraModule.CameraInput)
          v5.Connect("AimStateChanged", function(p8)
          	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1, (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_7
          	if p8 then
          		v_u_1.MouseDeltaSensitivity = v_u_3.Sett.AimSensSettings
          		v_u_7.setTouchSensitivityMultiplier(v_u_3.Sett.AimSensSettings)
          	else
          		v_u_1.MouseDeltaSensitivity = v_u_3.Sett.SensSettings
          		v_u_7.setTouchSensitivityMultiplier(v_u_3.Sett.SensSettings)
          	end
          end)
          if v_u_3 and v_u_3.Sett then
          	v_u_4.FieldOfView = v_u_3.Sett.fovSettings
          	v_u_1.MouseDeltaSensitivity = v_u_3.Sett.SensSettings
          	v_u_7.setTouchSensitivityMultiplier(v_u_3.Sett.SensSettings)
          end
          v_u_3.DataUpdated.Event:Connect(function(p9, _)
          	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_1, (copy) v_u_7

      function v_u_1.GiveTask(p3, p4) -- name: GiveTask
      	if p4 then
      		p3._tasks[p4] = p4
      		return p4
      	end

      end
      function v_u_1.Destroy(p5) -- name: Destroy
      	for v6, _ in pairs(p5._tasks) do
      		if typeof(v6) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
      			v6:Disconnect()
      		elseif typeof(v6) == "Instance" then
      			v6:Destroy()
      		elseif typeof(v6) == "function" then
      			v6()
      		elseif v6.Disconnect then
      			v6:Disconnect()
      		elseif v6.Destroy then
      			v6:Destroy()
      		end
      	end
      	table.clear(p5._tasks)
      end
      return v_u_1

  - [ModuleScript] NotifierBack
    --- [СКОПИРОВАННЫЙ КОД СРИПТА] ---
      local v_u_1 = game:GetService("RunService")
      local v2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
      local v_u_3 = game:GetService("TweenService")
      local v_u_4 = game:GetService("Players")
      game:GetService("Debris")
      local v_u_5 = v2:FindFirstChild("NotificationEvent")
      if not v_u_5 then
      	v_u_5 = Instance.new("RemoteEvent")
      	v_u_5.Name = "NotificationEvent"
      	v_u_5.Parent = v2
      end
      local v6 = {}
      local function v_u_24(p_u_7, p8, p_u_9) -- name: createClientNotification
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4, (copy) v_u_3
      	local v10 = v_u_4.LocalPlayer
      	if v10 then
      		local v11 = v10:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):WaitForChild(p8)
      		if v11 then
      			local v_u_12 = v11:WaitForChild("NotificationContainer")
      			local v_u_13 = v_u_12:WaitForChild("NotificationTemplate")
      			task.spawn(function()
      				-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_13, (copy) v_u_12, (copy) p_u_7, (copy) p_u_9, (ref) v_u_3
      				local v14 = v_u_13:Clone()
      				v14.Parent = v_u_12
      				v14.Visible = true
      				v14.Name = "Notification"
      				v14.Message.Text = p_u_7
      				v14.LayoutOrder = tick()
      				if p_u_9 then
      					local v15 = p_u_9
      					if type(v15) == "string" then
      						local v16 = Instance.new("Sound")
      						v16.SoundId = p_u_9
      						v16.Parent = v14
      						v16:Play()
      					end
      				end
      				local v17 = v_u_13.BackgroundTransparency
      				v14.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0)
      				v14.BackgroundTransparency = 1
      				v14.Message.TextTransparency = 1
      				local v18 = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
      				local v19 = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
      				local v20 = v_u_3:Create(v14, v18, {
      					["Size"] = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
      					["BackgroundTransparency"] = v17
      				})
      				local v21 = v_u_3:Create(v14.Message, v18, {
      					["TextTransparency"] = 0
      				})
      				v20:Play()
      				v21:Play()
      				task.wait(2)
      				local v22 = v_u_3:Create(v14, v19, {
      					["BackgroundTransparency"] = 1
      				})
      				local v23 = v_u_3:Create(v14.Message, v19, {
      					["TextTransparency"] = 1
      				})
      				v22:Play()
      				v23:Play()
      				v22.Completed:Wait()
      				v14:Destroy()
      			end)
      		else
      			warn("Notifier (Client): " .. p8 .. " not found in PlayerGui.")
      		end
      	else
      		return
      	end
      end
      function v6.notify(...) -- name: notify
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_1, (ref) v_u_5, (copy) v_u_24
      	local v25 = { ... }
      	if v_u_1:IsServer() then
      		local v26, v27, v28, v29 = table.unpack(v25)
      		if v26 and (v26:IsA("Player") and v27) then
      			v_u_5:FireClient(v26, v27, v28, v29)
      		else
      			warn("Notifier (Server): Incorrect arguments. Expected (player, message, [optional type], [optional soundId]).")
      		end
      	else
      		local v30, v31, v32 = table.unpack(v25)
      		if v30 and type(v30) == "string" then
      			if v31 == "small" then
      				v_u_24(v30, "SmallNotificationGui", v32)
      			else
      				v_u_24(v30, "NotificationGui", v32)
      			end
      		else
      			warn("Notifier (Client): Incorrect arguments. Expected (message, [optional type], [optional soundId]).")
      			return
      		end
      	end
      end
      local function v36(p33, p34, p35) -- name: onServerNotification
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_24
      	if p34 == "small" then
      		v_u_24(p33, "SmallNotificationGui", p35)
      	else
      		v_u_24(p33, "NotificationGui", p35)
      	end
      end
      if v_u_1:IsClient() and true then
      	local v37 = v_u_4.LocalPlayer
      	if not v37 then
      		return v6
      	end
      	local v38 = v37:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
      	for _, v39 in ipairs({ "NotificationGui", "SmallNotificationGui" }) do
      		if not v38:FindFirstChild(v39) then
      			local v40 = script:FindFirstChild(v39)
      			if v40 and v40:IsA("ScreenGui") then
      				v40:Clone().Parent = v38
      			else
      				warn("Notifier (Client): Could not find \'" .. v39 .. "\' ScreenGui inside the module script.")
      			end
      		end
      	end
      	v_u_5.OnClientEvent:Connect(v36)
      end
      return v6

      	end

      	["Force"] = function(p40, p41, p42, p43) -- name: Force
      		if p43.Gravity == 0 or p43.LiftSpeed then
      			for _, v44 in ipairs(p40:GetDescendants()) do
      				if v44:IsA("BasePart") then
      					local v45 = v44:GetMass()
      					local v46 = Instance.new("Attachment", v44)
      					local v47 = Instance.new("VectorForce", v44)
      					v47.Name = "AntiGravityForce"
      					v47.Attachment0 = v46
      					v47.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
      					local v48 = v45 * workspace.Gravity
      					local v49 = Vector3.new(0, v48, 0)
      					local v50 = v45 * (p43.LiftSpeed or 0)
      					v47.Force = v49 + Vector3.new(0, v50, 0)
      				end
      			end
      		end

      		local v52 = p40:FindFirstChild("IceBlock") or v51

      			if p42 then

      				v52:ApplyImpulse(((v54.Magnitude < 0.5 and Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) or v54.Unit) + Vector3.new(0, 0.35, 0)).Unit * v53 * (p43.Push or 0))
      			end
      			if p43.Spin then
      				local v55 = math.random() * 2 - 1
      				local v56 = math.random() * 2 - 1
      				local v57 = math.random() * 2 - 1
      				v52:ApplyAngularImpulse(Vector3.new(v55, v56, v57).Unit * v53 * p43.Spin)
      			end
      		end
      	end,
      	["PlaySound"] = function(p58, p59) -- name: PlaySound

      	["Shatter"] = function(p66, p67) -- name: Shatter

      	end,

      function v1.Ragdoll(p91, p92, p93) -- name: Ragdoll
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_90
      	local v94 = v_u_90.Spawn(p91, p92)
      	if v94 then
      		v_u_90.Rig(v94, "Floppy")
      		v_u_90.Force(v94, p92, p93, {
      			["Push"] = 100,
      			["Spin"] = 50
      		})
      		return v94
      	end
      end
      function v1.FrozenEffect(p95, p_u_96, p97) -- name: FrozenEffect
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_90
      	local v_u_98 = v_u_90.Spawn(p95, p_u_96)
      	if v_u_98 then
      		v_u_90.Rig(v_u_98, "Stiff")
      		v_u_90.Visuals(v_u_98, {
      			["Material"] = nil,
      			["Color"] = nil,
      			["Transparency"] = 0.3,
      			["Material"] = Enum.Material.Ice,
      			["Color"] = Color3.fromRGB(130, 215, 255)
      		})
      		local v99 = v_u_98:FindFirstChild("Torso") or v_u_98:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
      		local v_u_100
      		if v99 then
      			v_u_100 = Instance.new("Part", v_u_98)
      			v_u_100.Name = "IceBlock"
      			v_u_100.Size = Vector3.new(4.5, 6, 4.5)
      			v_u_100.CFrame = v99.CFrame
      			v_u_100.Material = Enum.Material.Ice
      			v_u_100.Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 230, 255)
      			v_u_100.Transparency = 0.4
      			v_u_90.SetCollision(v_u_100)
      			local v101 = Instance.new("WeldConstraint", v_u_100)
      			v101.Part0 = v_u_100
      			v101.Part1 = v99
      		else
      			v_u_100 = nil
      		end
      		v_u_90.Force(v_u_98, p_u_96, p97, {
      			["Push"] = 40,
      			["Spin"] = 30
      		})
      		task.delay(1.5, function()
      			-- upvalues: (ref) v_u_100, (copy) v_u_98, (copy) p_u_96, (ref) v_u_90
      			if v_u_100 then
      				v_u_100:Destroy()
      			end
      			local v102 = v_u_98.PrimaryPart and v_u_98.PrimaryPart.Position or p_u_96.Position
      			v_u_90.PlaySound("IceShatter", v102)
      			v_u_90.Shatter(v_u_98, {
      				["Material"] = nil,
      				["Force"] = 25,

      			})
      		end)
      	end
      end

      function v1.ExplodeEffect(p107, p108, _) -- name: ExplodeEffect
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_90
      	local v109 = v_u_90.Spawn(p107, p108)
      	if v109 then
      		for _, v110 in ipairs(v109:GetDescendants()) do
      			if v110:IsA("Motor6D") or v110:IsA("Weld") then
      				v110:Destroy()
      			end
      		end
      		local v111 = v109.PrimaryPart.Position
      		v_u_90.PlaySound("Explode", v111)
      		for _, v112 in ipairs(v109:GetDescendants()) do
      			if v112:IsA("BasePart") then
      				local v113 = (v112.Position - v111).Unit
      				local v114 = v113.Magnitude == 0 and Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) or v113
      				local v115 = math.random() - 0.5
      				local v116 = math.random()
      				local v117 = math.random() - 0.5
      				local v118 = Vector3.new(v115, v116, v117)
      				v112:ApplyImpulse((v114 + v118) * v112:GetMass() * 40)
      				v112:ApplyAngularImpulse(v118 * v112:GetMass() * 40)
      			end
      		end
      	end
      end

      function v1.BlackHoleEffect(p138, p139, _) -- name: BlackHoleEffect
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_90, (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_4
      	local v140 = v_u_90.Spawn(p138, p139)
      	if v140 then
      		v_u_90.Rig(v140, "Floppy")
      		local v_u_141 = v_u_90.GetAsset("BlackHole")
      		if v_u_141 then
      			local v142 = v140.PrimaryPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
      			v_u_141.Position = v142
      			v_u_141.Anchored = true
      			v_u_141.CanCollide = false
      			v_u_90.SetCollision(v_u_141)
      			v_u_141.Parent = workspace
      			v_u_3:AddItem(v_u_141, 2.5)
      			if v_u_141:IsA("Model") then
      				if v_u_141.PrimaryPart then
      					v_u_141:ScaleTo(0.1)
      					local v143 = Instance.new("NumberValue")
      					v143.Value = 0.1
      					v143.Parent = v_u_141
      					v_u_4:Create(v143, TweenInfo.new(1), {
      						["Value"] = 1
      					}):Play()
      					v143.Changed:Connect(function(p144)
      						-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_141
      						if v_u_141.Parent then
      							v_u_141:ScaleTo(p144)
      						end
      					end)
      				end
      			else
      				v_u_141.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
      				v_u_4:Create(v_u_141, TweenInfo.new(1), {
      					["Size"] = Vector3.new(6, 6, 6)
      				}):Play()
      			end
      			v_u_90.PlaySound("BlackHoleSound", v142)
      			for _, v145 in ipairs(v140:GetDescendants()) do
      				if v145:IsA("BasePart") then
      					v145.CanCollide = false
      					v145.Anchored = false
      					local v146 = Instance.new("Attachment", v145)
      					local v147
      					if v_u_141:IsA("Model") then
      						v147 = v_u_141.PrimaryPart or v_u_141
      					else
      						v147 = v_u_141
      					end
      					local v148 = Instance.new("Attachment", v147)
      					local v149 = Instance.new("LineForce", v145)
      					v149.Attachment0 = v146
      					v149.Attachment1 = v148
      					v149.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
      					v149.InverseSquareLaw = false
      					v149.Magnitude = 2000 * v145:GetMass()
      					v_u_4:Create(v145, TweenInfo.new(0.8), {
      						["Transparency"] = 1
      					}):Play()
      				end
      			end
      		else
      			warn("[DeathEffects] Missing \'BlackHole\' asset!")
      		end
      	else
      		return
      	end
      end

      				v_u_172.Anchored = false

      				v_u_172:ApplyAngularImpulse(Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1))

      					v_u_197:Destroy()

      							v199:Destroy()
      						end
      					end
      				end
      			end)
      		end

    ---------------------------------
  - [ModuleScript] SeasonConfig
    --- [СКОПИРОВАННЫЙ КОД СРИПТА] ---
      local v_u_4 = {
      	["SeasonStartTimestamp"] = os.time({
      		["year"] = 2026,
      		["month"] = 1,
      		["day"] = 9,
      		["hour"] = 0,
      		["min"] = 0,
      		["sec"] = 0
      	}),
      	["SeasonDuration"] = 5184000,
      	["GetCurrentSeasonInfo"] = function() -- name: GetCurrentSeasonInfo
      		-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_4
      		local v1 = os.time() - v_u_4.SeasonStartTimestamp
      		if v1 < 0 then
      			return 0, v_u_4.SeasonStartTimestamp
      		end
      		local v2 = v1 / v_u_4.SeasonDuration
      		local v3 = math.floor(v2) + 1
      		return v3, v_u_4.SeasonStartTimestamp + v3 * v_u_4.SeasonDuration
      	end
      }
      return v_u_4
    ---------------------------------
  - [ModuleScript] GridManager
    --- [СКОПИРОВАННЫЙ КОД СРИПТА] ---
      return {
      	["Update"] = function(p_u_1, p_u_2, p3) -- name: Update
      		if p_u_1 and p_u_2 then
      			local v4 = p_u_1.AbsoluteSize
      			local v5 = p_u_1:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")
      			local v6 = v5 and (v5.PaddingLeft.Offset or 0) or 0
      			local v7 = v5 and (v5.PaddingRight.Offset or 0) or 0
      			local v_u_8 = v5 and (v5.PaddingTop.Offset or 0) or 0
      			local v_u_9 = v5 and v5.PaddingBottom.Offset or 0
      			local v10 = v4.X - v6 - v7 - 2
      			local v11 = v4.Y - v_u_8 - v_u_9 - 2
      			if p_u_1:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
      				v10 = v10 - p_u_1.ScrollBarThickness
      			elseif p_u_1.Parent and p_u_1.Parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
      				v10 = v10 - p_u_1.Parent.ScrollBarThickness
      			end
      			if v10 > 0 then
      				local v12 = p3.Columns or 3
      				local v13 = p3.Rows
      				local v14 = p3.AspectRatio or 1
      				local v_u_15 = p3.Padding or 5
      				local v16 = p3.SizeScale or 1
      				local v17 = v12 / v16
      				local v18 = v13 and v13 / v16 or nil
      				p_u_2.CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(v_u_15, v_u_15)
      				p_u_2.FillDirectionMaxCells = v12
      				local v19 = (v10 - v_u_15 * (math.ceil(v17) - 1)) / v17
      				local v20 = not v18 and 99999 or (v11 - v_u_15 * (math.ceil(v18) - 1)) / v18
      				local v21 = v19 / v14
      				local v22 = v20 * v14
      				if v20 >= v21 then
      					v20 = v21
      					v22 = v19
      				end
      				p_u_2.CellSize = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(v22), (math.floor(v20)))
      				task.delay(0, function()
      					-- upvalues: (copy) p_u_1, (copy) p_u_2, (copy) v_u_15, (copy) v_u_8, (copy) v_u_9
      					if p_u_1 and p_u_2 then
      						local v23 = p_u_2.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + v_u_15 * 2 + v_u_8 + v_u_9
      						if p_u_1:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
      							p_u_1.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, v23)

      local function v_u_63(p59) -- name: getTagState
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_3, (copy) v_u_2

      		end
      	end

      end
      local function v_u_71(p64, p65, p66) -- name: toggleTag

      						v_u_2:RemoveTag(v69, v70)

      		end

      	end
      end

  - [LocalScript] PluginMove
    --- [СКОПИРОВАННЫЙ КОД СРИПТА] ---
      local v1 = plugin:CreateToolbar("Model Aligner"):CreateButton("Align Model to Part", "Select 1 Model and 1 Part. Moves, rotates, and scales the Model to match the Part.", "rbxassetid://150793763")
      local v_u_2 = game:GetService("Selection")
      local v_u_3 = game:GetService("ChangeHistoryService")
      v1.Click:Connect(function()
      	-- upvalues: (copy) v_u_2, (copy) v_u_3
      	local v4 = v_u_2:Get()
      	if #v4 == 2 then
      		local v5, v6
      		if v4[1]:IsA("Model") and v4[2]:IsA("BasePart") then
      			v5 = v4[1]
      			v6 = v4[2]
      		else
      			if not (v4[2]:IsA("Model") and v4[1]:IsA("BasePart")) then
      				warn("Aligner Plugin: You must select exactly one Model and one BasePart.")
      				return
      			end
      			v5 = v4[2]
      			v6 = v4[1]
      		end
      		v_u_3:SetWaypoint("Before Aligning Model")
      		v5:PivotTo(v6.CFrame)
      		local _, v7 = v5:GetBoundingBox()
      		local v8 = v6.Size.X / v7.X
      		local v9 = v6.Size.Y / v7.Y
      		local v10 = v6.Size.Z / v7.Z
      		local v11 = (v8 + v9 + v10) / 3
      		v5:ScaleTo(v5:GetScale() * v11)
      		v_u_3:SetWaypoint("After Aligning Model")
      		print("Aligner Plugin: Model successfully moved, rotated, and scaled to the Part!")
      	else
      		warn("Aligner Plugin: Please select exactly one Model and one BasePart.")
      	end
      end)
